{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"In the repo /home/kali/sigil, perform an EXHAUSTIVE census of every call site that scans the append-only spine, for a hard-prune state-folding audit. This is a completeness-critical enumeration — missing one is a potential tamper-evidence hole.\n\nSearch the entire `sigil/` package (and note any in `apps/`, `tools/`, `bridge/`, `mcp/`, `mesh/` etc.) for EVERY call to any of these on a SpineStore or spine records:\n  - `.iter_records(` and `.iter_records()` (with and without a `since_seq=` argument)\n  - `.entries(` / `.entries()`\n  - `.count(`, `.next_seq`, `.get(`, `.resolve(`\n  - any `for r in store` / direct iteration over spine records\n  - any place that reads ALL records from seq 0 (a full scan) vs. a windowed scan from a cursor\n\nFor EACH call site, report in a compact table with columns:\n  1. `file:line`\n  2. the enclosing function/method\n  3. whether it is WINDOWED (passes `since_seq=` / a cursor, so it only reads NEW records — prune-safe) or a FULL SCAN from genesis\n  4. a one-line classification of WHAT it computes, into exactly one of these buckets:\n     - `WINDOWED` — reads from a cursor, prune-safe, no fold needed\n     - `MONOTONIC-SECURITY-BEARER` — accumulates a security-relevant monotonic state over ALL history (nonce highwater, kill-switch latch, device authorization/revocation, promotion grants, creation-cap counts, warden anchor, approval-pending set, archivist current-view, gesture/arm replay ledger, budget day-cap). THESE MUST FOLD over a pruned prefix.\n     - `CONTENT-LOOKUP` — resolves or returns specific record content by seq/id (would need `resolve()`→PrunedRef/410 handling if it can reference a pruned seq)\n     - `TOTALS/DISPLAY` — counts or totals for a graph/dashboard/report\n\nPay special attention to and explicitly confirm presence/absence + location of the folding bearers named in the design: `envelope.device_nonce_highwater`, warden checkpoint scan, `killswitch._scan_engaged`, `actor_scope.creation_allowed`, `mesh.authorized_devices` + `capability_map`, `promotion.is_promoted`, `approvals.pending`, the archivist `revise`/`iter_current` current-view feeding `queries.py` (open_threads / due_commitments / pending_contradictions), `gesture/session` device-arm nonce replay ledger, `governor/budget` per-UTC-day cap.\n\nBe thorough — check multiple naming conventions and grep broadly. Report the full table plus a final list of exactly which call sites are MONOTONIC-SECURITY-BEARERs (the fold list) and which are CONTENT-LOOKUPs (the resolve() list). Do not modify any files."},"uuid":"7213252b-91be-4ba4-a13c-068933c8e97f","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:20:42.371Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f96e6b74-3c1a-47a7-b6ac-f01c73b87d56","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. {"parentUuid":"5a6fbe6d-a8ca-4697-a4ee-998aebea597f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019WWxro1s4a6kAodkh9r5hY","type":"tool_result","content":"727\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n728\t        \"\"\"Records with seq > `since_seq`, in seq order, ACROSS ALL SEGMENTS (sealed segments then the\n729\t        active) as ONE contiguous chain, so every full-scanning consumer keeps seeing the whole log. For\n730\t        since_seq >= 0 the index seeks straight to the first wanted line in its owning segment\n731\t        (O(records-returned), not O(spine)); a full read (since_seq < 0) streams every segment from the\n732\t        first. A line that fails to parse or lacks required keys is SKIPPED (a torn active tail can't crash\n733\t        the read; a torn MIDDLE line becomes a seq gap that verify() fails on). RAISES SpineError if the\n734\t        manifest references a segment that cannot be read — a read must NEVER silently yield a short chain\n735\t        (invariant 15: no state-scanner fail-open).\"\"\"\n736\t        yielded_upto = since_seq\n737\t        for _attempt in range(6):                        # D2 reader-grace: tolerate a few compaction moves\n738\t            segs = self._segments_in_order()             # RAISES on a genuinely missing referenced segment\n739\t            if not segs:\n740\t                return\n741\t            start_i, start_off = self._start_locus(yielded_upto, segs)\n742\t            try:\n743\t                for i in range(start_i, len(segs)):\n744\t                    p = segs[i]\n745\t                    with self._open_segment(p) as f:\n746\t                        if i == start_i and start_off:\n747\t                            f.seek(start_off)\n748\t                        for raw in f:\n749\t                            if not raw.strip():\n750\t                                continue\n751\t                            try:\n752\t                                rec = SpineRecord.from_dict(json.loads(raw))\n753\t                            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n754\t                                _log.warning(\"spine: skipping malformed line during iter_records (%s)\", p)\n755\t                                continue\n756\t                            if rec.seq > yielded_upto:\n757\t                                yield rec\n758\t                                yielded_upto = rec.seq\n759\t                return                                    # completed cleanly\n760\t            except FileNotFoundError:\n761\t                # a concurrent compaction moved a plaintext segment to trash AFTER we resolved it — re-read\n762\t                # the manifest (now pointing at the .gz) and continue from where we left off. Retain-all\n763\t                # means the same seqs are still covered, so no record is skipped or repeated.\n764\t                _log.debug(\"spine: a segment moved during read (compaction); re-resolving from seq %d\", yielded_upto)\n765\t                continue\n766\t        raise SpineError(\"spine segments kept moving during read (compaction churn) — giving up\")\n767\t\n768\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n769\t        \"\"\"A single record by seq via the segment-aware index (resolves the OWNING segment + offset),\n770\t        byte-identical to a full scan. O(1) for a plaintext segment; for a gzip-sealed segment the offset is\n771\t        a DECOMPRESSED offset, so `gzip.seek()` decompresses up to it (bounded by the seal threshold — not a\n772\t        full-spine scan). Falls back to a bounded forward scan (spanning segments) when the seq is not\n773\t        indexed (beyond the tip, or a corrupt line was skipped).\"\"\"\n774\t        if seq < 0:\n775\t            return None\n776\t        self._ensure_index()\n777\t        with self._index_lock:\n778\t            loc = self._offsets.get(seq)\n779\t        if loc is not None:\n780\t            path_str, off = loc\n781\t            try:\n782\t                with self._open_segment(Path(path_str)) as f:\n783\t                    f.seek(off)\n784\t                    raw = f.readline()\n785\t                rec = SpineRecord.from_dict(json.loads(raw))\n786\t                if rec.seq == seq:\n787\t                    return rec\n788\t            except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n789\t                pass                                     # stale/moved offset — fall through to the scan\n790\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n791\t            if r.seq == seq:\n792\t                return r\n793\t            if r.seq > seq:\n794\t                break\n795\t        return None\n796\t\n797\t    def tail(self, n: int) -> list[SpineRecord]:\n798\t        \"\"\"The last `n` records, walking segments backward from the ACTIVE across the seam (O(n) bytes,\n799\t        NOT O(spine)) — a bounded RECENT-window read on a large spine. Fewer than `n` if the spine is\n800\t        shorter. FIXES B3: the pre-Slice-1 version read only the active file, so right after a rotation it\n801\t        returned < n. NOTE (unchanged): a bounded window collapses a rapid recent replay flood but does NOT\n802\t        bound AGGREGATE replay bloat — pair `tail()`-based dedup with a record-time freshness gate.\"\"\"\n803\t        if n <= 0:\n804\t            return []\n805\t        for _attempt in range(6):                        # D2 reader-grace: a concurrent compaction may\n806\t            try:                                         # unlink a plaintext between resolve and read\n807\t                recs: list[SpineRecord] = []\n808\t                for p in reversed(self._segments_in_order()):\n809\t                    recs = self._read_segment_tail(p, n - len(recs)) + recs\n810\t                    if len(recs) >= n:\n811\t                        break\n812\t                return recs[-n:]\n813\t            except FileNotFoundError:\n814\t                _log.debug(\"spine: a segment moved during tail() (compaction); re-resolving\")\n815\t                continue\n816\t        raise SpineError(\"spine segments kept moving during tail() (compaction churn) — giving up\")\n817\t\n818\t    def _read_segment_tail(self, p: Path, k: int) -> list[SpineRecord]:\n819\t        \"\"\"The last `k` records of a single segment. Plaintext: seek-from-end (O(k) bytes). Gzip: decompress\n820\t        (bounded by the seal threshold) and take the last k lines — no random access into a gz stream.\n821\t        Opening a segment that was resolved from the manifest but has since VANISHED (a concurrent\n822\t        compaction unlinked the plaintext) RAISES FileNotFoundError so tail() re-resolves — NOT a silent []\n823\t        (which would drop a whole segment from the window and hole the chain; the arm replay-dedup gate\n824\t        depends on a complete window).\"\"\"\n825\t        if k <= 0:\n826\t            return []\n827\t        if p.suffix == \".gz\":\n828\t            with gzip.open(p, \"rb\") as f:                # raises FileNotFoundError if it vanished -> tail() retries\n829\t                lines = [x for x in f.read().split(b\"\\n\") if x.strip()]\n830\t        else:\n831\t            with p.open(\"rb\") as f:                      # raises FileNotFoundError if it vanished -> tail() retries\n832\t                f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n833\t                pos = f.tell()\n834\t                buf = b\"\"\n835\t                while pos > 0 and buf.count(b\"\\n\") <= k:\n836\t                    step = min(65536, pos)\n837\t                    pos -= step\n838\t                    f.seek(pos)\n839\t                    buf = f.read(step) + buf\n840\t            lines = [x for x in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if x.strip()]\n841\t        out: list[SpineRecord] = []\n842\t        for ln in lines[-k:]:\n843\t            try:\n844\t                out.append(SpineRecord.from_dict(json.loads(ln)))\n845\t            except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n846\t                continue\n847\t        return out\n848\t\n849\t    @property\n850\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n851\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n852\t\n853\t    def count(self) -> int:\n854\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n855\t\n856\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n857\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n858\t        return [\n859\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n860\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n861\t        ]\n862\t\n863\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n864\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n865\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n866\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n867\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n868\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n869\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n870\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n871\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n872\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n873\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n874\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n875\t            content = {\n876\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n877\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n878\t            }\n879\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n880\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n881\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n882\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n883\t\n884\t    @staticmethod\n885\t    def _entry_from_line(line: str | None) -> \"ChainEntry | None\":\n886\t        if not line:"}]},"uuid":"3672830d-1387-4d6c-8801-fc3d22acf65b","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:21:21.828Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a6fbe6d-a8ca-4697-a4ee-998aebea597f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e6195b19-6d79-4d59-97d4-b89d09ba0aad","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VAUDW4yQjiBxktxkcFRnDp","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Device-signed, replay-resistant request envelope (Phase 9 W1-A) — the bridge auth keystone.\n2\t\n3\tThe phone (a device the owner AUTHORIZED via `sigil.mesh.authorize_device`) signs EACH request to the\n4\tdesktop bridge with ITS OWN Ed25519 key over the canonical envelope core. There is NO wire bearer\n5\tsecret: authentication IS the signature, verified against the owner-minted authorized-device set. This\n6\tmirrors the approval-record pattern (`agents.approvals._approval_message` / `verify_approval`) — same\n7\tcanonical-JSON message, same fail-closed `verify_one`, same trust pinned to owner-minted device keys\n8\t(a rogue/revoked device cannot self-authorize; a tampered field breaks the signature).\n9\t\n10\tReplay resistance is a deterministic, spine-anchored monotonic-nonce highwater PER DEVICE, enforced\n11\tATOMICALLY for EFFECTFUL requests: the check-then-receipt runs under the spine lock (re-entrant), so\n12\tconcurrent replays of one captured envelope can't both pass. An effectful request must carry a nonce\n13\tstrictly greater than the highest nonce that device has ever been receipted for. Reads are authenticated\n14\tby signature alone (the transport verifies them without a receipt and bounds their replay with a\n15\ttimestamp window). The receipt is an append-only spine event, so the watermark is tamper-evident memory.\n16\t\n17\tThis module is PURE by construction — NO wallclock, NO RNG. The caller supplies `nonce` and `ts` so the\n18\tJS phone client and Python reproduce the exact signed bytes (the parity contract) and so freshness is a\n19\tdeterministic function of the spine. A wallclock timestamp-WINDOW check (is `ts` recent?) belongs to\n20\tthe server transport layer, not here. Offense-free.\"\"\"\n21\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n22\t\n23\tfrom typing import Set, Tuple, Union\n24\t\n25\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sign, verify_one\n26\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n27\t\n28\t# The request vocabulary the bridge accepts — a fail-closed allow-list. `panic`/`relay` are effectful;\n29\t# the `read:*` family is side-effect-free. An action outside this set is refused even if validly signed.\n30\tACTIONS = frozenset({\"panic\", \"relay\", \"read:snapshot\", \"read:pending\", \"read:record\",\n31\t                     \"read:stream\", \"read:recall\"})\n32\t\n33\t# The spine signal marking a consumed request. Its records form the per-device nonce highwater.\n34\tRECEIPT_SIGNAL = \"mesh.request\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef envelope_message(core: dict) -> bytes:\n38\t    \"\"\"The exact bytes signed/verified — canonical JSON of the core. This is the JS<->Python parity\n39\t    contract: a future JS client MUST produce these identical bytes. Mirrors\n40\t    `agents.approvals._approval_message` (return bytes; encode only if `canonical_json` gave a str).\"\"\"\n41\t    m = canonical_json(core)\n42\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef build_core(device_pubkey: str, action: str, args: dict, nonce, ts) -> dict:\n46\t    \"\"\"The signed core. `nonce`/`ts` are caller-supplied (this module never reads a clock or RNG).\"\"\"\n47\t    return {\"v\": 1, \"device\": device_pubkey, \"action\": action, \"args\": args, \"nonce\": nonce, \"ts\": ts}\n48\t\n49\t\n50\tdef sign_envelope(device_key, core: dict) -> dict:\n51\t    \"\"\"Phone/test side: attach a DEVICE signature over the canonical core, producing the wire payload.\"\"\"\n52\t    return {**core, \"sig\": sign(device_key.private_key_b64, envelope_message(core))}\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef verify_envelope(payload, authorized: Set[str]) -> Tuple[bool, Union[dict, str]]:\n56\t    \"\"\"Fail-closed authentication. Rebuild the core (payload minus `sig`); refuse if there is no sig /\n57\t    no device / the device is not in the owner-minted authorized set; then Ed25519-verify the signature\n58\t    over the canonical core; finally require the (now-authenticated) action to be in the `ACTIONS`\n59\t    allow-list. Mirrors `agents.approvals.verify_approval`. Returns (True, core) or (False, reason).\"\"\"\n60\t    p = payload.payload if hasattr(payload, \"payload\") else payload\n61\t    sig = p.get(\"sig\")\n62\t    device = p.get(\"device\")\n63\t    if not sig or not device or not isinstance(device, str):   # a non-str device would crash the `in authorized` set test\n64\t        return False, \"missing/invalid signature or device\"\n65\t    if device not in authorized:                       # trust pinned to owner-minted device keys\n66\t        return False, \"device is not authorized\"\n67\t    core = {k: v for k, v in p.items() if k != \"sig\"}\n68\t    try:                                               # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig\n69\t        sig_ok = verify_one(device, envelope_message(core), sig)\n70\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any crypto/decode error is a fail-closed refusal, never a crash\n71\t        return False, \"signature invalid\"\n72\t    if not sig_ok:\n73\t        return False, \"signature invalid\"\n74\t    if core.get(\"action\") not in ACTIONS:              # allow-list the authenticated action (fail-closed)\n75\t        return False, f\"unknown action: {core.get('action')!r}\"\n76\t    return True, core\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tdef record_receipt(store: SpineStore, core: dict) -> int:\n80\t    \"\"\"Append the append-only consumption receipt. Carries only the routing fields — never `args`\n81\t    (which may hold a subject) and never the signature; the record is auto-tier (an A0 event).\"\"\"\n82\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n83\t                        payload={\"signal\": RECEIPT_SIGNAL, \"device\": core[\"device\"],\n84\t                                 \"action\": core[\"action\"], \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"],\n85\t                                 \"ts\": core[\"ts\"], \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tdef device_nonce_highwater(store: SpineStore, device_pubkey: str) -> int:\n89\t    \"\"\"The highest receipted nonce for this device (-1 if none, so a first nonce of 0 is valid). Non-int\n90\t    nonces are tolerated (skipped).\"\"\"\n91\t    hi = -1\n92\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n93\t        p = r.payload\n94\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == RECEIPT_SIGNAL and p.get(\"device\") == device_pubkey:\n95\t            nonce_raw = p.get(\"nonce\")\n96\t            if nonce_raw is None:                          # skip receipts with no recorded nonce\n97\t                continue\n98\t            try:\n99\t                n = int(nonce_raw)\n100\t            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n101\t                continue\n102\t            if n > hi:\n103\t                hi = n\n104\t    return hi\n105\t\n106\t\n107\tdef consume(store: SpineStore, payload, authorized: Set[str], *, effectful: bool) -> dict:\n108\t    \"\"\"Authenticate, gate replay for effectful requests, receipt, return the core. Raises `ValueError`\n109\t    with the refusal reason on any failure (fail-closed). An effectful request requires a nonce strictly\n110\t    fresher than this device's highwater; a read skips that gate but still receipts (advancing it).\"\"\"\n111\t    ok, core = verify_envelope(payload, authorized)\n112\t    if not ok:\n113\t        raise ValueError(core)\n114\t    assert isinstance(core, dict)                          # verify_envelope returns the core dict when ok\n115\t    n_raw = core.get(\"nonce\")\n116\t    if n_raw is None:                                      # missing nonce → invalid (was: int(None) TypeError below)\n117\t        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n118\t    if isinstance(n_raw, float) and n_raw != n_raw:        # NaN (n != n) — canonical-json serializes it, so guard here\n119\t        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n120\t    try:\n121\t        nonce = int(n_raw)                                 # int(inf) raises OverflowError (NOT a ValueError) — catch it\n122\t    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):\n123\t        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n124\t    if nonce < 0:\n125\t        raise ValueError(\"invalid nonce\")\n126\t    if effectful:\n127\t        # ATOMIC check-then-receipt: hold the re-entrant spine lock across the highwater read AND the\n128\t        # receipt append, so two concurrent replays of one captured envelope can't both pass (TOCTOU).\n129\t        from ..spine.store import spine_lock\n130\t        with spine_lock(store.path):\n131\t            if nonce <= device_nonce_highwater(store, core[\"device\"]):\n132\t                raise ValueError(\"replay: nonce not fresh\")\n133\t            record_receipt(store, core)\n134\t    else:\n135\t        record_receipt(store, core)\n136\t    return core\n137\t"}]},"uuid":"8170e23f-c4db-4de9-ac6e-62fd1e68ed96","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:22:26.570Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e6195b19-6d79-4d59-97d4-b89d09ba0aad","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"13f6cc13-fd74-4b10-a7f9-2abd6e52b5f4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M1hga7q1EkcNn6Rb88Nznd","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The kill switch (SIGIL §5) — a single latch that halts the agent mesh while leaving perception and\n2\tmemory-read (A0 observe) alive. State is append-only on the spine.\n3\t\n4\tASYMMETRIC AUTHENTICATION (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2): halting is always the safe direction, so ANY\n5\tengage event halts (a nuisance forged-engage is at worst a fail-safe DoS). UN-halting is the\n6\tdangerous direction, so a RELEASE is honored ONLY if it is signed by the owner key and verifies\n7\tagainst the trusted pubkey — a forged release can never revive a halted mesh. Read fresh per decision\n8\tso a release (or engage) takes effect immediately.\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport threading\n12\tfrom pathlib import Path\n13\tfrom typing import Optional\n14\t\n15\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n16\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n17\t\n18\tSIGNAL = \"governor.killswitch\"\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\")\n20\t\n21\t# FIX 4 (audit CRITICAL): `is_engaged()` full-scans the spine on EVERY governor decision, so a batch of\n22\t# proposals is O(proposals × spine). Cache the authoritative verdict keyed by (resolved spine path,\n23\t# trusted pubkey) with the store's ROTATION-AWARE CHANGE TOKEN it was computed at. Every engage/release\n24\t# APPENDS a record → the token changes → the cache invalidates and we re-run the real, owner-signed-\n25\t# release-verifying scan. A matching token ⇒ no new records ⇒ the cached verdict is exact. The token\n26\t# (invariant 9 / A4) keys on the manifest generation + the resolved ACTIVE segment (size, inode), NOT a\n27\t# bare `store.path.stat()` — which would raise/freeze once a migration renames spine.jsonl away and then\n28\t# serve a STALE (un-halting) verdict indefinitely. Keyed on the pubkey too, so instances with different\n29\t# trust roots never share an entry. Shared across ALL callers on a path.\n30\t_STATE_CACHE: dict[tuple[str, Optional[str]], tuple[tuple, bool]] = {}\n31\t_CACHE_GUARD = threading.Lock()\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tclass KillSwitch:\n35\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n36\t        self.store = store\n37\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n38\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n39\t\n40\t    def engage(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n41\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\"}\n42\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n43\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n44\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n45\t\n46\t    def release(self, *, by: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n47\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"released\"}\n48\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": by, \"reason\": reason,\n49\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n50\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n51\t\n52\t    def is_engaged(self) -> bool:\n53\t        \"\"\"Cheap, correct kill-switch verdict (FIX 4). Cheap when the spine is unchanged since the last\n54\t        authoritative scan (a matching rotation-aware change token); a changed token — a new engage/release\n55\t        record, or a migration/rotation that moved the active segment — re-runs the real scan below and\n56\t        refreshes the shared cache. Semantics are IDENTICAL to a fresh scan, and (unlike a bare file-size\n57\t        check) a migration that renames spine.jsonl away can never freeze the token and serve a stale\n58\t        un-halting verdict.\"\"\"\n59\t        key = (str(Path(self.store.path).resolve()), self.trusted_pubkey)\n60\t        token = self.store.change_token()\n61\t        with _CACHE_GUARD:\n62\t            cached = _STATE_CACHE.get(key)\n63\t            if cached is not None and cached[0] == token:\n64\t                return cached[1]\n65\t        engaged = self._scan_engaged()\n66\t        with _CACHE_GUARD:\n67\t            _STATE_CACHE[key] = (token, engaged)\n68\t        return engaged\n69\t\n70\t    def _scan_engaged(self) -> bool:\n71\t        \"\"\"The AUTHORITATIVE scan — unchanged semantics (not re-implemented): honor ANY engage (halting\n72\t        is fail-safe), and un-halt only on an OWNER-SIGNED release that verifies (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n73\t        engaged = False\n74\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n75\t            p = r.payload\n76\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL:\n77\t                continue\n78\t            state = p.get(\"state\")\n79\t            if state == \"engaged\":\n80\t                engaged = True                      # honor ANY engage — halting is fail-safe\n81\t            elif state == \"released\" and verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n82\t                engaged = False                     # only an OWNER-SIGNED release un-halts (fail-closed)\n83\t        return engaged\n84\t"}]},"uuid":"7de68df3-b289-408c-a71c-90f05da1afcf","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:22:35.898Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"13f6cc13-fd74-4b10-a7f9-2abd6e52b5f4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9307a35c-9b74-4e8b-b588-5e8b585a1efa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PLkimKZKud3brzaHuhccph","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ActorScope (Phase 8, WS-G G-iv) — the origin allowlist that bounds where DELEGATE may act (the\n2\towner's OWN service integrations), mirroring `OperatorScope`: EMPTY = deny-all. A step's origin must\n3\tbe (1) http/https, (2) public (`sources.is_public_host` — no internal hosts), (3) in the allowlist\n4\t(scheme+host+port — port confusion refused). Plus a `creation_cap` (default 1) bounding account\n5\tcreations per service label AND per ORIGIN (relabelling the same origin cannot mint extra accounts) —\n6\tmass account creation is out of doctrine; the cap is checked at preview AND re-checked at execute.\"\"\"\n7\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n8\t\n9\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n10\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n11\t\n12\tfrom .sources import is_public_host\n13\t\n14\t_DEFAULT_PORT = {\"http\": 80, \"https\": 443}\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef _origin(url: str) -> Optional[str]:\n18\t    \"\"\"Canonical scheme://host:port (default port made explicit) — so `https://h` and `https://h:443`\n19\t    match, but `https://h:8443` does NOT match `https://h` (port confusion is refused).\"\"\"\n20\t    try:\n21\t        p = urlsplit(url if \"//\" in url else \"https://\" + url)\n22\t        if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname:\n23\t            return None\n24\t        port = p.port or _DEFAULT_PORT[p.scheme]\n25\t    except ValueError:                      # malformed / out-of-range port → fail-closed\n26\t        return None\n27\t    return f\"{p.scheme}://{p.hostname.lower()}:{port}\"\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tclass ActorScope:\n31\t    def __init__(self, allowed_origins: Optional[List[str]] = None, *, creation_cap: int = 1):\n32\t        self.allowed = {o for o in (_origin(a) for a in (allowed_origins or [])) if o}\n33\t        self.creation_cap = creation_cap\n34\t\n35\t    def origin_allowed(self, url: str) -> bool:\n36\t        try:\n37\t            p = urlsplit(url)\n38\t            if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname or not is_public_host(p.hostname):\n39\t                return False\n40\t        except ValueError:                  # malformed (e.g. bad IPv6 literal) → fail-closed, never crash the caller\n41\t            return False\n42\t        return bool(self.allowed) and _origin(url) in self.allowed\n43\t\n44\t    def creation_allowed(self, store, service: str, url: str = \"\") -> bool:\n45\t        \"\"\"True while fewer than `creation_cap` accounts have been created for this service label OR at\n46\t        this origin — so relabelling the same origin cannot outrun the cap.\"\"\"\n47\t        origin = _origin(url) if url else None\n48\t        n = 0\n49\t        for r in store.iter_records():\n50\t            p = r.payload\n51\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.step\" and p.get(\"step_kind\") == \"account.create\"\n52\t                    and p.get(\"status\") == \"applied\"\n53\t                    and (p.get(\"service\") == service\n54\t                         or (origin is not None and _origin(p.get(\"url\", \"\")) == origin))):\n55\t                n += 1\n56\t        return n < self.creation_cap\n57\t"}]},"uuid":"dd740c9d-d890-4d02-9189-a9d77f166560","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:22:41.346Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9307a35c-9b74-4e8b-b588-5e8b585a1efa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f66ceb87-f236-4bf8-8992-8d63f4ad9a77","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Kr4mkzFbcVNbSpYXbH3uGo","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The mesh registry (Phase 7, WS-D). Two owner-signed ledgers on the one spine:\n2\t\n3\t  • host_capability — each host advertises {os, screen, camera, gpu_vlm, always_on}, owner-signed, so\n4\t    an agent can route work to the best-capable ONLINE host from cited, tamper-evident records.\n5\t  • device authorization — a 1-of-N device ledger: the owner AUTHORIZES a phone's own Ed25519 device\n6\t    key with a signed `device_authorized` record (and `device_revoked` to remove it). The phone then\n7\t    approves A2/A3 items by signing with ITS key; `verify_approval(..., extra_pubkeys=authorized)`\n8\t    accepts it. Trust is pinned to owner-MINTED keys (RP-APPROVAL-2); the phone never holds the\n9\t    trust-root key, and can approve offline.\n10\t\n11\tAll reads are fail-closed: a descriptor/authorization that does not verify against the owner key is\n12\tignored. Reuses `governor.authn` (the same signed-event primitive kill/promotion/approval use).\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\tfrom typing import Optional, Set\n16\t\n17\tfrom ..agents.approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n18\tfrom ..agents.approvals import _approval_message\n19\tfrom ..governor.authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n20\tfrom ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n21\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex, sign\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tCAP_SIGNAL = \"mesh.host_capability\"\n25\tDEV_SIGNAL = \"mesh.device\"\n26\t_CAP_CORE = (\"signal\", \"host_id\", \"os\", \"has_screen\", \"has_camera\", \"has_gpu_vlm\", \"always_on\",\n27\t             \"has_hid_inject\", \"has_camera_stream\")\n28\t_DEV_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"device_id\", \"device_pubkey\")\n29\t\n30\t\n31\t# --- host capability advertisement -----------------------------------------------------------------\n32\tdef advertise_capability(store: SpineStore, descriptor: dict, owner_key) -> int:\n33\t    core = {\"signal\": CAP_SIGNAL, \"host_id\": descriptor[\"host_id\"], \"os\": descriptor[\"os\"],\n34\t            \"has_screen\": bool(descriptor[\"has_screen\"]), \"has_camera\": bool(descriptor[\"has_camera\"]),\n35\t            \"has_gpu_vlm\": bool(descriptor[\"has_gpu_vlm\"]), \"always_on\": bool(descriptor[\"always_on\"]),\n36\t            \"has_hid_inject\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_hid_inject\", False)),\n37\t            \"has_camera_stream\": bool(descriptor.get(\"has_camera_stream\", False))}\n38\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n39\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef capability_map(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n43\t    \"\"\"Latest VERIFIED capability per host_id (forged/unsigned advertisements ignored).\"\"\"\n44\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n45\t    latest: dict = {}\n46\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n47\t        p = r.payload\n48\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == CAP_SIGNAL and verify_signed(p, _CAP_CORE, tp):\n49\t            latest[p.get(\"host_id\")] = {k: p.get(k) for k in _CAP_CORE if k != \"signal\"}\n50\t    return latest\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t# --- device authorization ledger -------------------------------------------------------------------\n54\tdef authorize_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n55\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"authorized\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n56\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n57\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef revoke_device(store: SpineStore, device_id: str, device_pubkey: str, owner_key) -> int:\n61\t    core = {\"signal\": DEV_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"device_id\": device_id, \"device_pubkey\": device_pubkey}\n62\t    payload = {**signed_payload(core, owner_key), \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n63\t    return store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"OWNER\", payload=payload)\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef authorized_devices(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> Set[str]:\n67\t    \"\"\"The set of currently-authorized device pubkeys (owner-signed authorize, minus later revoke).\n68\t    An unsigned/forged authorization is ignored — a rogue device cannot self-authorize.\"\"\"\n69\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n70\t    state: dict = {}\n71\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n72\t        p = r.payload\n73\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == DEV_SIGNAL and p.get(\"state\") in (\"authorized\", \"revoked\") \\\n74\t                and verify_signed(p, _DEV_CORE, tp):\n75\t            state[p.get(\"device_pubkey\")] = p[\"state\"]      # latest verified state per device key\n76\t    return {pub for pub, st in state.items() if st == \"authorized\"}\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tclass DeviceApprover:\n80\t    \"\"\"Signs an approval with a DEVICE key (the phone). The record verifies via\n81\t    `verify_approval(record, owner_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized_devices(store))` — i.e. only while\n82\t    the device remains authorized. Never touches the owner trust-root key.\"\"\"\n83\t    def __init__(self, store: SpineStore, *, device_key):\n84\t        self.store = store\n85\t        self.device_key = device_key\n86\t\n87\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, approver: str, reason: str) -> int:\n88\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n89\t        sig = sign(self.device_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n90\t        payload = {\"signal\": _APPROVAL_SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq,\n91\t                   \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason, \"pubkey\": self.device_key.public_key_b64,\n92\t                   \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg), \"device\": True, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n93\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\", payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n94\t\n95\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n96\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver, reason)\n97\t\n98\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"device\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n99\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver, reason)\n100\t"}]},"uuid":"48791dea-1ac2-40ea-9b12-5d7ddc660e78","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:22:45.949Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f66ceb87-f236-4bf8-8992-8d63f4ad9a77","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e9f6b8ef-742b-456a-acf3-36527deec4d6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VgVHenQwusS6v8rjam6yjc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scope promotion policy (SIGIL §5). An owner may promote a specific (agent, scope) so its A2\n2\tproposals auto-approve instead of queuing — EXCEPT ENVOY, which has NO promotion path (§4.6). A3\n3\tnever promotes.\n4\t\n5\tAUTHENTICATED (Phase 6 red-pen RP-2/RP-4): a grant/revoke is meaningless unless SIGNED by the owner\n6\tkey and verified against the persisted trusted pubkey. `is_promoted` ignores any grant that does not\n7\tverify — so a forged grant written by a prompt-injected agent via `self.store` grants nothing. The\n8\tgate scope is the proposal's RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label\n9\t(RP-3), so a promotion is \"agent X may auto-approve A2 records of kind K.\" \"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom typing import Optional\n13\t\n14\tfrom .authn import signed_payload, verify_signed\n15\tfrom .identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n16\t\n17\tSIGNAL = \"governor.promotion\"\n18\tNO_PROMOTION_AGENTS = frozenset({\"ENVOY\", \"DELEGATE\"})   # outbound + account actions stay human-gated forever\n19\t_CORE = (\"signal\", \"state\", \"agent\", \"scope\")\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tclass PromotionPolicy:\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, *, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None):\n24\t        self.store = store\n25\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n26\t        self.trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n27\t\n28\t    def grant(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\"):\n29\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n30\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\",\n31\t                              payload={\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"agent\": agent,\n32\t                                       \"scope\": scope, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n33\t                                       \"reason\": f\"{agent} has no promotion path (SIGIL §4.6) — outbound stays human-gated\"})\n34\t            return None\n35\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"granted\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n36\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n37\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n38\t\n39\t    def revoke(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> int:\n40\t        core = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"state\": \"revoked\", \"agent\": agent, \"scope\": scope}\n41\t        payload = {**signed_payload(core, self.owner_key), \"by\": \"owner\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n42\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"WARDEN\", payload=payload)\n43\t\n44\t    def is_promoted(self, agent: str, scope: str = \"*\") -> bool:\n45\t        if agent in NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS:\n46\t            return False                          # never — structural, not policy\n47\t        state: dict = {}\n48\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n49\t            p = r.payload\n50\t            if p.get(\"signal\") != SIGNAL or p.get(\"state\") not in (\"granted\", \"revoked\"):\n51\t                continue\n52\t            if not verify_signed(p, _CORE, self.trusted_pubkey):\n53\t                continue                          # fail-closed: an unsigned/forged grant grants nothing\n54\t            state[(p.get(\"agent\"), p.get(\"scope\"))] = p[\"state\"]   # latest verified wins\n55\t        return state.get((agent, scope)) == \"granted\" or state.get((agent, \"*\")) == \"granted\"\n56\t"}]},"uuid":"7205e26b-d129-4811-9eca-43260f27b928","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:22:52.460Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e9f6b8ef-742b-456a-acf3-36527deec4d6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5352bb24-6934-44e6-ad93-fd3825c0dfca","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SKk6L7QcCvu86cbwNb9LSt","type":"tool_result","content":"150\t\n151\t\n152\tdef _as_float(v: object) -> float:\n153\t    \"\"\"As `_as_int`, CLAMPED to >= 0.0 (a negative cost must not cancel spend — fail-open), and mapping a\n154\t    non-finite value (NaN/±inf) to 0.0 (a forged NaN cost would sum to NaN and slip the `cost >= cap`\n155\t    check). Catches OverflowError too: a forged huge-INTEGER cost (a 400-digit JSON int, which json.loads\n156\t    returns as a Python int, not inf) would otherwise raise `float()`→OverflowError and CRASH the gate —\n157\t    bricking the agent's dispatch for the whole UTC day and crashing `sigil budget`.\"\"\"\n158\t    if isinstance(v, (int, float, str)):\n159\t        try:\n160\t            f = float(v)\n161\t        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):\n162\t            return 0.0\n163\t        if not math.isfinite(f) or f < 0.0:\n164\t            return 0.0\n165\t        return f\n166\t    return 0.0\n167\t\n168\t\n169\tclass BudgetLedger:\n170\t    def __init__(self, store, caps: Optional[BudgetCaps] = None):\n171\t        self.store = store\n172\t        self.caps = caps or BudgetCaps()\n173\t\n174\t    def spent(self, agent: str, day_iso: str) -> Spend:\n175\t        \"\"\"Today's spend for `agent`: actions, interrupt events, tokens (input+output), and USD cost —\n176\t        all counted from the agent's own records for the UTC date `day_iso` (the ts is informational,\n177\t        so bucketing is by wallclock date). Token/cost come from `payload[\"usage\"]`, derived the SAME\n178\t        way as actions so there is nothing separate to drift or forge.\"\"\"\n179\t        actions = interrupts = tokens = 0\n180\t        cost = 0.0\n181\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n182\t            if r.source != \"agent\" or r.actor != agent:\n183\t                continue\n184\t            if not (r.ts or \"\").startswith(day_iso):\n185\t                continue\n186\t            # count only actions actually TAKEN (auto/queued) — a denial consumes no budget, so a flood\n187\t            # of denied attempts can't self-reinforce the cap. A denied record carries no `usage` (the\n188\t            # refusal record has none), so this same gate keeps token/cost fail-closed too.\n189\t            if r.payload.get(\"decision\") not in (\"auto\", \"queued\"):\n190\t                continue\n191\t            actions += 1\n192\t            if r.kind == \"event\":\n193\t                interrupts += 1\n194\t            u = r.payload.get(\"usage\")\n195\t            if isinstance(u, dict):\n196\t                tokens += _as_int(u.get(\"input_tokens\")) + _as_int(u.get(\"output_tokens\"))\n197\t                cost += _as_float(u.get(\"cost_usd\"))\n198\t        return Spend(actions=actions, interrupts=interrupts, tokens=tokens, cost_usd=cost)\n199\t\n200\t    def over_budget(self, agent: str, day_iso: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n201\t        \"\"\"Fail-closed: at/over ANY configured cap → (True, reason). All caps None → never over (opt-in;\n202\t        default dispatch unchanged). A denied action wrote no `usage`, so it never advances the meter.\"\"\"\n203\t        caps = self.caps\n204\t        if (caps.daily_actions is None and caps.daily_interrupts is None\n205\t                and caps.daily_tokens is None and caps.daily_cost_usd is None):\n206\t            return False, \"\"\n207\t        s = self.spent(agent, day_iso)\n208\t        if caps.daily_actions is not None and s.actions >= caps.daily_actions:\n209\t            return True, f\"daily action cap ({caps.daily_actions}) reached for {agent}\"\n210\t        if caps.daily_interrupts is not None and s.interrupts >= caps.daily_interrupts:\n211\t            return True, f\"daily interrupt cap ({caps.daily_interrupts}) reached for {agent}\"\n212\t        if caps.daily_tokens is not None and s.tokens >= caps.daily_tokens:\n213\t            return True, f\"daily token cap ({caps.daily_tokens}) reached for {agent}\"\n214\t        if caps.daily_cost_usd is not None and s.cost_usd >= caps.daily_cost_usd:\n215\t            return True, f\"daily cost cap (${caps.daily_cost_usd:g}) reached for {agent}\"\n216\t        return False, \"\"\n217\t\n218\t    def report(self, day_iso: str) -> dict[str, dict]:\n219\t        \"\"\"Per-agent {actions, interrupts, tokens, cost_usd} for `day_iso` (UTC date prefix) — the\n220\t        read-only view behind `sigil budget`. Single spine scan; counts only actions actually TAKEN\n221\t        (auto/queued), mirroring the enforced ledger, so a denial never inflates the report.\"\"\"\n222\t        out: dict[str, dict] = {}\n223\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n224\t            if r.source != \"agent\":\n225\t                continue\n226\t            if not (r.ts or \"\").startswith(day_iso):\n227\t                continue\n228\t            if r.payload.get(\"decision\") not in (\"auto\", \"queued\"):\n229\t                continue\n230\t            a = out.setdefault(r.actor, {\"actions\": 0, \"interrupts\": 0, \"tokens\": 0, \"cost_usd\": 0.0})\n231\t            a[\"actions\"] += 1\n232\t            if r.kind == \"event\":\n233\t                a[\"interrupts\"] += 1\n234\t            u = r.payload.get(\"usage\")\n235\t            if isinstance(u, dict):\n236\t                a[\"tokens\"] += _as_int(u.get(\"input_tokens\")) + _as_int(u.get(\"output_tokens\"))\n237\t                a[\"cost_usd\"] += _as_float(u.get(\"cost_usd\"))\n238\t        for a in out.values():\n239\t            a[\"cost_usd\"] = round(a[\"cost_usd\"], 6)\n240\t        return out\n241\t"}]},"uuid":"6ca8e2fc-5f21-41ad-8935-8cb156d65c69","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:16.239Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5352bb24-6934-44e6-ad93-fd3825c0dfca","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ca1782b4-41e9-468b-bf33-333f9fafab06","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Heis6PRzSThjNXaj2NW9UT","type":"tool_result","content":"40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"e9bea2f8-6129-4421-9398-fa733c56a2a0","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:26.261Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ca1782b4-41e9-468b-bf33-333f9fafab06","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"a15c9dca-d8bd-4245-addf-af884c57bb6f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JyZQtFMEZ7arTPwHSJBSnr","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only supersession + current-view read (ported from agents/blackboard.py's\n2\tpost/supersede/read discipline). The spine is never edited; a revised fact is SUPERSEDED by\n3\ta later record whose `supersedes_id` names it. `iter_current` returns the live view — the\n4\tconsolidation records that nothing later has superseded — which is what the MCP tools serve.\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom typing import Iterator\n8\t\n9\tfrom ..spine.models import SpineRecord\n10\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n11\tfrom .grounding import CONSOLIDATE_SOURCE\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef consolidation_records(store: SpineStore, kinds: set[str] | None = None) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n15\t    \"\"\"Every record the ARCHIVIST wrote (source=archivist), optionally filtered by kind.\"\"\"\n16\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n17\t        if r.source == CONSOLIDATE_SOURCE and (kinds is None or r.kind in kinds):\n18\t            yield r\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef iter_current(store: SpineStore, kinds: set[str] | None = None) -> list[SpineRecord]:\n22\t    \"\"\"The live view: consolidation records of `kinds` that nothing later has superseded.\"\"\"\n23\t    recs = list(consolidation_records(store, kinds))\n24\t    superseded = {r.supersedes_id for r in recs if r.supersedes_id is not None}\n25\t    return [r for r in recs if r.seq not in superseded]\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef promotion_ledgers(store: SpineStore) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:\n29\t    \"\"\"Two SEPARATE idempotency ledgers: keys already promoted as GROUNDED facts, and keys\n30\t    already recorded as demoted refusals. Kept apart so a prior DEMOTE never blocks a later\n31\t    GROUNDED promotion of the same fact — demote-only must not become demote-permanent.\"\"\"\n32\t    grounded: set[str] = set()\n33\t    refused: set[str] = set()\n34\t    for r in consolidation_records(store):\n35\t        k = r.payload.get(\"promotion_key\")\n36\t        if k is None:\n37\t            continue\n38\t        (refused if r.kind == \"refusal\" else grounded).add(k)\n39\t    return grounded, refused\n40\t"}]},"uuid":"bb668273-62a9-4258-b6c6-f9f35bcc6200","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:31.937Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a15c9dca-d8bd-4245-addf-af884c57bb6f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":988,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdC7uN39tyNnGuEcz6e5Z","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"ad623722-7786-4622-a04a-8bdc6e562119","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:39.069Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ad623722-7786-4622-a04a-8bdc6e562119","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdC7uP98fVtCiYiw1rzSL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TjpBKNGrmxm8SQyDTXgKWz","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/consolidate/queries.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":988,"cache_read_input_tokens":48623,"output_tokens":417,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":988},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":417,"cache_read_input_tokens":48623,"cache_creation_input_tokens":988,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":988,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdC7uN39tyNnGuEcz6e5Z","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"34dd3b9e-a37c-461b-b01c-f0afb6511275","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:39.333Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"34dd3b9e-a37c-461b-b01c-f0afb6511275","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TjpBKNGrmxm8SQyDTXgKWz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Read helpers that power the 3 consolidation MCP tools and the nightly brief. All serve\n2\tONLY grounded, current (non-superseded) records, each carrying its spine citation.\"\"\"\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\tfrom typing import Any\n6\t\n7\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n8\tfrom .grounding import belief_mean, is_grounded\n9\tfrom .revise import iter_current\n10\t\n11\t\n12\tdef _cite(r) -> dict[str, Any]:\n13\t    p = r.payload\n14\t    # `text` is the AUTHORITATIVE grounded fact — the verbatim record quote, negation and word\n15\t    # order intact. The model's `statement` is served only as an advisory `summary` (never the\n16\t    # fact). Cite the VERIFIED subset (what actually re-executed), not the model's full claim.\n17\t    return {\"seq\": r.seq, \"entry_hash\": r.entry_hash, \"when\": r.ts,\n18\t            \"subject\": p.get(\"subject\"), \"text\": p.get(\"quote\"), \"summary\": p.get(\"statement\"),\n19\t            \"source_seqs\": p.get(\"verified_seqs\") or p.get(\"source_seqs\"), \"grounding\": p.get(\"grounding\"),\n20\t            \"belief\": round(belief_mean(p.get(\"alpha\", 1), p.get(\"beta\", 1)), 3)}\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tdef _grounded_current(store: SpineStore, kinds: set[str]) -> list:\n24\t    return [r for r in iter_current(store, kinds) if is_grounded(r.payload.get(\"grounding\"))]\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef open_threads(store: SpineStore, limit: int = 25) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n28\t    \"\"\"The owner's live loops: current grounded decisions + commitments, most-stale first\n29\t    (oldest source record), each cited. Superseded/resolved records are already excluded.\"\"\"\n30\t    recs = _grounded_current(store, {\"decision\", \"commitment\"})\n31\t    recs.sort(key=lambda r: (min(r.payload.get(\"source_seqs\") or [r.seq]), r.seq))  # oldest = stalest\n32\t    out = []\n33\t    for r in recs[:limit]:\n34\t        d = _cite(r)\n35\t        d[\"kind\"] = r.kind\n36\t        if r.kind == \"commitment\":\n37\t            d[\"owner\"] = r.payload.get(\"owner\")\n38\t            d[\"due\"] = r.payload.get(\"due_iso\")\n39\t        out.append(d)\n40\t    return out\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef due_commitments(store: SpineStore, *, before_iso: str | None = None, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n44\t    \"\"\"Current grounded commitments that carry a due date, earliest-due first. Deduped by\n45\t    (subject, owner) to the LATEST record, so a rescheduled deadline serves the new due, not a\n46\t    stale one. `before_iso` (explicit → deterministic) filters to those due on/before it.\"\"\"\n47\t    recs = [r for r in _grounded_current(store, {\"commitment\"}) if r.payload.get(\"due_iso\")]\n48\t    # keep the CHRONOLOGICALLY latest promise per (subject, owner). Recency is the source-record\n49\t    # chronology (max cited seq), NOT the promoted record's own seq — that follows the extractor's\n50\t    # emit order, so a reschedule emitted first would otherwise wrongly serve the stale due (finding 1).\n51\t    def _recency(r):\n52\t        return (max(r.payload.get(\"source_seqs\") or [r.seq]), r.seq)\n53\t    latest: dict[tuple, Any] = {}\n54\t    for r in recs:\n55\t        k = (str(r.payload.get(\"subject\", \"\")).strip().lower(), str(r.payload.get(\"owner\", \"\")).strip().lower())\n56\t        if k not in latest or _recency(r) > _recency(latest[k]):\n57\t            latest[k] = r\n58\t    recs = list(latest.values())\n59\t    if before_iso:\n60\t        recs = [r for r in recs if str(r.payload.get(\"due_iso\")) <= before_iso]\n61\t    recs.sort(key=lambda r: (str(r.payload.get(\"due_iso\")), r.seq))\n62\t    out = []\n63\t    for r in recs[:limit]:\n64\t        d = _cite(r)\n65\t        d[\"owner\"] = r.payload.get(\"owner\")\n66\t        d[\"due\"] = r.payload.get(\"due_iso\")\n67\t        out.append(d)\n68\t    return out\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tdef pending_contradictions(store: SpineStore, limit: int = 25) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n72\t    \"\"\"Current, unresolved self-contradictions — extractor-JUDGED opposition, gate-verified,\n73\t    flagged never adjudicated. Each names the conflicting record seqs so the owner can resolve\n74\t    them, and carries the verbatim quote that grounded the flag.\"\"\"\n75\t    recs = [r for r in iter_current(store, {\"contradiction\"})\n76\t            if not r.payload.get(\"resolved\") and is_grounded(r.payload.get(\"grounding\"))]\n77\t    recs.sort(key=lambda r: r.seq)\n78\t    return [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"entry_hash\": r.entry_hash, \"when\": r.ts,\n79\t             \"subject\": r.payload.get(\"subject\"),\n80\t             \"conflicting_seqs\": r.payload.get(\"conflicting_seqs\"),\n81\t             \"detail\": r.payload.get(\"statement\"), \"quote\": r.payload.get(\"quote\")}\n82\t            for r in recs[:limit]]\n83\t"}]},"uuid":"c5c20a18-59ea-4a5a-b4f2-634d070fe05b","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:39.375Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"34dd3b9e-a37c-461b-b01c-f0afb6511275","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6095a7fd-0b88-4c52-812d-e7c67a393be3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YS93BY6z9nVaa48mMgK2M1","type":"tool_result","content":"40\t\n41\tINTENT_TOOL = {\n42\t    \"move\": \"hid.pointer.move\", \"click\": \"hid.pointer.click\",\n43\t    \"scroll_left\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\", \"scroll_right\": \"hid.pointer.scroll\",\n44\t    \"drag\": \"hid.pointer.drag\", \"type\": \"hid.type\", \"combo\": \"hid.combo\", \"launch\": \"hid.app.launch\",\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef pending_device_arms(store, trusted_pubkey=None):\n49\t    \"\"\"ALL RECORDED device arm requests (written by the bridge's `submit_arm_request`) that have NOT yet\n50\t    armed a session, OLDEST-FIRST — candidates for `arm_by_device`, which RE-VERIFIES each fully (auth /\n51\t    freshness / kill-switch / replay / single-session / TTL). Returns a list (possibly empty). Oldest-first\n52\t    + returning ALL candidates means a newer STALE request never shadows an older still-valid one. This is\n53\t    the wiring that makes the remote-arm path reachable end-to-end (the gesture daemon consumes it).\"\"\"\n54\t    armed = set()\n55\t    cands = []\n56\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n57\t        p = r.payload\n58\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\":\n59\t            armed.add((p.get(\"device_pubkey\"), p.get(\"nonce\")))\n60\t        elif (p.get(\"signal\") == ARM_REQUEST and p.get(\"decision\") == \"auto\"\n61\t              and p.get(\"sig\") and p.get(\"pubkey\")):\n62\t            cands.append(dict(p))\n63\t    return [c for c in cands if (c.get(\"pubkey\"), c.get(\"nonce\")) not in armed]\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tdef arm_request_message(core: dict) -> bytes:\n67\t    \"\"\"The canonical bytes a device signs to request an arm — ONLY the signed core fields, so a caller\n68\t    cannot smuggle unsigned extras. Mirrors the approval/envelope canonical-json contract.\"\"\"\n69\t    from ..reuse import canonical_json\n70\t    m = canonical_json({k: core.get(k) for k in _ARM_CORE})\n71\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode(\"utf-8\")\n72\t\n73\t\n74\tdef sign_arm_request(device_key, *, device_id: str, nonce, ts: float, ttl_seconds: float) -> dict:\n75\t    \"\"\"Phone-side: build a device-signed arm request. The device signs with its OWN key (never the owner\n76\t    trust-root); the owner authorized this device once via the mesh ledger. This is the credential the\n77\t    bridge records and `SessionGate.arm_by_device` verifies.\"\"\"\n78\t    from ..reuse import sign\n79\t    core = {\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": device_id, \"nonce\": nonce, \"ts\": ts, \"ttl_seconds\": ttl_seconds}\n80\t    return {**core, \"pubkey\": device_key.public_key_b64,\n81\t            \"sig\": sign(device_key.private_key_b64, arm_request_message(core))}\n82\t\n83\t\n84\t@dataclass\n85\tclass Session:\n86\t    session_id: str\n87\t    live: bool = True\n88\t    expires_at: float = 0.0            # wallclock deadline — a session is BOUNDED, never indefinite\n89\t\n90\t    def expired(self, now: float) -> bool:\n91\t        return self.expires_at > 0.0 and now >= self.expires_at\n92\t\n93\t\n94\tclass SessionGate:\n95\t    def __init__(self, store, backend: InputBackend, *, classifier=None, owner_key=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n96\t        self.store = store\n97\t        self.backend = backend\n98\t        self._classifier = classifier\n99\t        self._owner_key = owner_key\n100\t        self._trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey\n101\t        self.session: Optional[Session] = None\n102\t        self._ks_engaged = False\n103\t        self._ks_token: tuple | None = None   # last-scanned spine change token; None forces a fresh check first\n104\t        self._ks_checked_at = -1e9\n105\t\n106\t    def _trusted(self):\n107\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n108\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n109\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n110\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n111\t\n112\t    def _killswitch_engaged(self, now: float) -> bool:\n113\t        \"\"\"Kill-switch state for the per-intent gate. Re-scans the AUTHORITATIVE `KillSwitch` (which\n114\t        verifies the owner-signed release — never re-implemented here) ONLY when the store's ROTATION-AWARE\n115\t        change token has moved since the last scan: a panic APPENDS a record → the token changes → the halt\n116\t        is honored within ~1-2 frames (≈66 ms worst case), not a fixed 0.5 s. A pure-movement gesture\n117\t        appends nothing, so the token is unchanged and this is cheap with NO scan. Using the change token\n118\t        (invariant 9 / A4) rather than `store.path.stat()` is what keeps a panic observable AFTER a\n119\t        migration — a bare size check would raise/freeze once spine.jsonl is renamed away, silently"}]},"uuid":"5c03d98a-a40c-4f69-a1f1-c78e119e9430","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:23:49.610Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6095a7fd-0b88-4c52-812d-e7c67a393be3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
