{"parentUuid":"0cfeb8fb-8021-4dd5-8f34-41bf8d214159","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VnyWx2op2yJyVFDDYgDqEp","type":"tool_result","content":"195\t        try:                                            # verify_one RAISES on a malformed-length sig — treat as refusal\n196\t            sig_ok = bool(pub) and pub in authorized and verify_one(pub, arm_request_message(core), request.get(\"sig\", \"\"))\n197\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — any crypto/decoding error is a fail-closed refusal, never a crash\n198\t            sig_ok = False\n199\t        if not sig_ok:\n200\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"unauthorized or invalid device signature\")\n201\t            return None\n202\t        # (3) freshness — bounds the replay window and rejects a stale captured request. Written\n203\t        #     fail-CLOSED for non-finite input: `nan > 30` is False, so a signed NaN ts would slip a\n204\t        #     `>`-style gate; require finite AND within-window explicitly.\n205\t        try:\n206\t            # request is a caller-supplied dict; a missing/None ts hits the except below (fail-closed)\n207\t            ts = float(request.get(\"ts\"))  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n208\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n209\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"missing/invalid timestamp\")\n210\t            return None\n211\t        if not math.isfinite(ts) or not (abs(now - ts) <= ARM_FRESHNESS):\n212\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"stale or non-finite timestamp\")\n213\t            return None\n214\t        # (4) replay — this (device, nonce) must not have armed before (the append-only spine is witness).\n215\t        nonce = request.get(\"nonce\")\n216\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n217\t            p = r.payload\n218\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == SESSION_ARMED and p.get(\"armed_by\") == \"device\"\n219\t                    and p.get(\"device_pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"nonce\") == nonce):\n220\t                self._refuse_arm(request, \"replayed arm nonce\")\n221\t                return None\n222\t        # (5) single live session — a device arm NEVER displaces an existing/owner session.\n223\t        if self.session is not None and self.session.live and not self.session.expired(now):\n224\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"a gesture session is already live\")\n225\t            return None\n226\t        # (6) TTL clamp — shorter than a local owner arm; a deliberate trust-narrowing inside the widening.\n227\t        #     Reject non-finite/non-positive BEFORE the min() (min(nan,300)=nan → a never-expiring session).\n228\t        try:\n229\t            raw_ttl = float(request.get(\"ttl_seconds\", MAX_DEVICE_TTL))\n230\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n231\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"invalid ttl\")\n232\t            return None\n233\t        if not math.isfinite(raw_ttl) or raw_ttl <= 0:\n234\t            self._refuse_arm(request, \"non-finite or non-positive ttl\")\n235\t            return None\n236\t        ttl = min(raw_ttl, MAX_DEVICE_TTL)\n237\t        sid = uuid.uuid4().hex\n238\t        # The record's AUTHORIZATION is the verified device signature — self-verifying vs the owner-signed\n239\t        # device ledger; actor=DEVICE mirrors submit_device_approval. Store the ORIGINAL SIGNED core\n240\t        # fields (so the sig re-verifies for ANY ttl, incl. a clamped one) + the effective TTL separately,\n241\t        # and ONLY known fields (never `**request` — no unsigned extras persisted).\n242\t        self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n243\t                          payload={\"signal\": SESSION_ARMED, \"session_id\": sid, \"armed_by\": \"device\",\n244\t                                   \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"], \"device_pubkey\": pub,\n245\t                                   \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"], \"ts\": core[\"ts\"],\n246\t                                   \"ttl_seconds\": core[\"ttl_seconds\"],   # ORIGINAL signed value → sig re-verifies\n247\t                                   \"effective_ttl\": ttl,                 # the CLAMPED ttl actually enforced\n248\t                                   \"sig\": request.get(\"sig\"), \"pubkey\": pub, \"authorization\": \"device-signed\",\n249\t                                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n250\t                                   \"summary\": \"gesture session ARMED by authorized device (remote)\"})\n251\t        self.session = Session(sid, live=True, expires_at=now + ttl)\n252\t        return self.session\n253\t\n254\t    def _refuse_arm(self, request: dict, reason: str) -> None:\n255\t        self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"gesture\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n256\t                          payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"decision\": \"refused\", \"tier\": \"A0\",\n257\t                                   \"device_id\": request.get(\"device_id\"), \"reason\": reason,\n258\t                                   \"summary\": f\"device gesture arm refused: {reason}\"})\n259\t        return None\n260\t\n261\t    # --- Layer 2: per-intent tier gate ------------------------------------------------------------\n262\t    def handle(self, intent: GestureIntent) -> dict:\n263\t        \"\"\"Authorize + act on ONE intent. Returns a verdict dict. Injection ONLY inside a live session\n264\t        and ONLY for A1 tools; A2+ queues for approval and injects NOTHING; a danger name → refused.\"\"\""}]},"uuid":"859732ae-09bb-469c-ab13-faf4ab4c4a56","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:24:02.078Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0cfeb8fb-8021-4dd5-8f34-41bf8d214159","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c78fe4fb-70ca-4e5a-8809-439022790de6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HGWdsJViB16G71ook4Xf2Z","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SpineTailer (Phase 7, P0.2) — a live tail over the append-only, poll-only spine with an HONEST,\n2\ttwo-layer integrity model (red-pen-hardened: unkeyed hashes alone cannot resist a writer who can\n3\trecompute them):\n4\t\n5\t  • `integrity_ok` (CHEAP, per-poll, unkeyed) — per-record binding+derivation (`verify_record`) plus\n6\t    cross-record LINKAGE (this record's `prev_hash` == the last emitted `entry_hash`). This catches\n7\t    ACCIDENTAL corruption, a naive stale-field tamper, a deletion/reorder/gap. It does NOT resist a\n8\t    file-writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash (a tip tamper or a forward-cascaded fork stays\n9\t    internally consistent) — so it is necessary but not sufficient, and is never presented as proof\n10\t    of authenticity on its own.\n11\t\n12\t  • `anchored` (KEYED, via `check_anchor`) — the owner-SIGNED head (`checkpoint.classify_head` /\n13\t    `verify_head`, Ed25519 1-of-1 trust root, monotonic `last_seq`) notarizes the prefix\n14\t    [0 .. signed_last_seq]. A recompute-fork or truncation AT OR BELOW the signed head is\n15\t    unforgeable without the owner key and is caught here; a monotonic high-water catches a rollback\n16\t    of even the un-notarized tail. Records with seq > signed_last_seq are the FRESH, not-yet-keyed\n17\t    tail — emitted `anchored=False` (well-formed but not tamper-proof), keyed at the next `sigil sign`.\n18\t\n19\tA consumer (UI/mobile) shows `integrity_ok=False` as \"integrity broken\", and treats only\n20\t`anchored=True` records as owner-notarized truth; the un-notarized tail is labelled as such, never\n21\tdressed up as proven. One primitive, two consumers — the UI SSE feed and the mobile push notifier —\n22\tboth drive `poll()` on a tick and fan events out via `Broadcaster`.\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\tfrom collections import deque\n26\tfrom typing import Optional\n27\t\n28\tfrom ..config import HEAD_PATH\n29\tfrom ..reuse import SignedChainHead\n30\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV\n31\tfrom .checkpoint import _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA, classify_head, trust_root\n32\tfrom .floor import load_floor\n33\tfrom .store import SpineStore\n34\tfrom .verify import verify_record\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _peek_schema(raw: str) -> int:\n38\t    \"\"\"Lenient read of a head's schema_version even when the strict model rejects it (a future-schema head\n39\t    carries fields `extra=\"forbid\"` refuses). 1 on any parse failure.\"\"\"\n40\t    try:\n41\t        import json\n42\t        return int(json.loads(raw).get(\"schema_version\", 1))\n43\t    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):\n44\t        return 1\n45\t\n46\t\n47\tdef _shape(record, *, integrity_ok: bool, integrity_reason: str, anchored: bool) -> dict:\n48\t    \"\"\"The cited atom shape emitted to consumers. `integrity_ok` = unkeyed well-formedness;\n49\t    `anchored` = covered by the owner-signed head (keyed). `text` is best-effort, never fabricated.\"\"\"\n50\t    return {\n51\t        \"seq\": record.seq, \"kind\": record.kind, \"source\": record.source, \"actor\": record.actor,\n52\t        \"ts\": record.ts, \"entry_hash\": record.entry_hash, \"text\": record.text(),\n53\t        \"integrity_ok\": integrity_ok, \"integrity_reason\": integrity_reason, \"anchored\": anchored,\n54\t    }\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tclass SpineTailer:\n58\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: int = -1,\n59\t                 signed_head: Optional[SignedChainHead] = None, tr=None):\n60\t        \"\"\"Emit records with seq > `since_seq`. `signed_head`/`tr` may be injected (tests); otherwise\n61\t        the owner-signed head is loaded from `HEAD_PATH` + the persisted trust root.\"\"\"\n62\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t        self.cursor = since_seq\n64\t        if since_seq < 0:\n65\t            self._last_hash = _GENESIS_PREV\n66\t        else:\n67\t            anchor = self.store.get(since_seq)\n68\t            self._last_hash = anchor.entry_hash if anchor else _GENESIS_PREV\n69\t        self._injected_head = signed_head\n70\t        self._injected_tr = tr\n71\t        self._high_water = -1                           # monotonic anti-rollback water mark\n72\t        self._signed_last_seq = -1\n73\t        self.check_anchor()                             # establish the initial keyed anchor (reads disk)\n74\t\n75\t    def poll(self) -> list[dict]:\n76\t        \"\"\"Newly-appended records (seq > cursor), each with the cheap `integrity_ok` verdict and the\n77\t        `anchored` (keyed) flag from the last `check_anchor`. On an integrity failure the event is\n78\t        emitted flagged (not dropped); the cursor still advances so the feed never re-emits or stalls.\"\"\"\n79\t        out: list[dict] = []\n80\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=self.cursor):\n81\t            atom_ok, reason = verify_record(r)\n82\t            if r.prev_hash != self._last_hash:\n83\t                ok = False\n84\t                reason = f\"linkage break at seq {r.seq}: prev_hash != last emitted entry_hash (rewind/fork/gap)\"\n85\t            else:\n86\t                ok = atom_ok\n87\t            out.append(_shape(r, integrity_ok=ok, integrity_reason=reason,\n88\t                              anchored=(r.seq <= self._signed_last_seq)))\n89\t            self.cursor = r.seq\n90\t            self._last_hash = r.entry_hash\n91\t        return out\n92\t\n93\t    def check_anchor(self) -> dict:\n94\t        \"\"\"The KEYED verdict. Verify the owner-signed head over the current chain (unforgeable without\n95\t        the owner key): a recompute-fork or truncation at/below the signed head → `anchor_ok=False`;\n96\t        a monotonic high-water rollback of even the un-notarized tail → `rollback` set. Updates\n97\t        `signed_last_seq` so `poll()` can flag records beyond it as the un-notarized tail.\"\"\"\n98\t        entries = self.store.entries()                  # FRESH read from disk (not the cached head)\n99\t        head_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else -1\n100\t        rollback = None\n101\t        if head_seq < self._high_water:\n102\t            rollback = f\"ROLLBACK: on-disk head seq {head_seq} < monotonic high-water {self._high_water}\"\n103\t        else:\n104\t            self._high_water = head_seq\n105\t        head, tr, status = self._resolve_head()\n106\t        if status == \"ahead\":\n107\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n108\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback, \"upgrade_required\": True,\n109\t                    \"reason\": \"signed head schema is newer than this build understands — upgrade sigil \"\n110\t                              \"(NOT treated as clean)\"}\n111\t        if head is None:\n112\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n113\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n114\t                    \"reason\": \"no signed checkpoint — the live tail is well-formed but UN-NOTARIZED \"\n115\t                              \"(run `sigil sign` to anchor)\"}\n116\t        try:\n117\t            floor = load_floor()                            # None if absent -> byte-identical to pre-floor\n118\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a PRESENT-but-corrupt floor is suspicious; fail CLOSED, never clean\n119\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n120\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n121\t                    \"reason\": f\"durable anti-rollback floor unreadable ({e})\"}\n122\t        try:\n123\t            ok, reason = classify_head(head, entries, tr, floor=floor)\n124\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a malformed head must FAIL closed, never crash the feed\n125\t            self._signed_last_seq = -1\n126\t            return {\"anchor_ok\": False, \"signed_last_seq\": -1, \"rollback\": rollback,\n127\t                    \"reason\": f\"anchor unverifiable ({e})\"}\n128\t        self._signed_last_seq = head.last_seq if ok else -1\n129\t        return {\"anchor_ok\": ok and rollback is None, \"signed_last_seq\": self._signed_last_seq,\n130\t                \"rollback\": rollback, \"reason\": reason}\n131\t\n132\t    def _resolve_head(self):\n133\t        \"\"\"(head, trust_root, status) where status is 'ok' | 'none' | 'ahead'. A head whose schema_version\n134\t        exceeds what this build understands returns status='ahead' (surfaced by check_anchor as\n135\t        anchor_ok=False + upgrade_required) — NEVER the benign 'un-notarized' degrade, which would be a\n136\t        false-CLEAN for a too-new head. A v1/v2 head returns 'ok' (byte-identical path).\"\"\"\n137\t        if self._injected_head is not None:\n138\t            return self._injected_head, self._injected_tr, \"ok\"\n139\t        try:\n140\t            if not HEAD_PATH.exists():\n141\t                return None, None, \"none\"\n142\t            raw = HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n143\t            try:\n144\t                head = SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(raw)\n145\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a future-schema head fails extra=\"forbid\"; distinguish it\n146\t                return None, None, (\"ahead\" if _peek_schema(raw) > _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA else \"none\")\n147\t            if head.schema_version > _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA:\n148\t                return None, None, \"ahead\"\n149\t            return head, trust_root(), \"ok\"\n150\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — no/broken head → unkeyed live tail (honest, fail-closed)\n151\t            return None, None, \"none\"\n152\t\n153\t\n154\tclass Broadcaster:\n155\t    \"\"\"Fan-out of tail events to N subscribers with bounded per-subscriber queues. On overflow the\n156\t    oldest events are dropped and the subscriber is marked `lagged` so it resyncs from the cursor\n157\t    (never a fabricated 'you saw everything').\"\"\"\n158\t    def __init__(self, maxlen: int = 512):\n159\t        self.maxlen = maxlen\n160\t        self._subs: dict[int, deque] = {}\n161\t        self._lagged: dict[int, bool] = {}\n162\t        self._next_id = 0\n163\t\n164\t    def subscribe(self) -> int:\n165\t        sid = self._next_id\n166\t        self._next_id += 1\n167\t        self._subs[sid] = deque(maxlen=self.maxlen)\n168\t        self._lagged[sid] = False\n169\t        return sid\n170\t\n171\t    def unsubscribe(self, sid: int) -> None:\n172\t        self._subs.pop(sid, None)\n173\t        self._lagged.pop(sid, None)\n174\t\n175\t    def publish(self, events: list[dict]) -> None:\n176\t        for sid, q in self._subs.items():\n177\t            for ev in events:\n178\t                if len(q) == self.maxlen:        # would drop the oldest → the subscriber lagged\n179\t                    self._lagged[sid] = True\n180\t                q.append(ev)\n181\t\n182\t    def drain(self, sid: int) -> tuple[list[dict], bool]:\n183\t        \"\"\"Return (events, lagged) and clear both. `lagged=True` tells the consumer to resync.\"\"\"\n184\t        q = self._subs.get(sid)\n185\t        if q is None:\n186\t            return [], False\n187\t        events = list(q)\n188\t        q.clear()\n189\t        lagged = self._lagged.get(sid, False)\n190\t        self._lagged[sid] = False\n191\t        return events, lagged\n192\t"}]},"uuid":"7c7e613b-1727-4220-aeef-472daf3fc6ea","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:24:10.735Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c78fe4fb-70ca-4e5a-8809-439022790de6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"4a53d6f1-91e7-402d-bb03-1c769d5060e0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdC7xMH6XGUbpwHwxzqNd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01C938rwaFJZBHRmCgr4bk6P","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/checkpoint.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4629,"cache_read_input_tokens":56436,"output_tokens":681,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4629},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":681,"cache_read_input_tokens":56436,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4629,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":4629,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdC7xHoFEgbNZdBtMVePS","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"ff57f5d9-700d-4879-8668-6796efff5dcb","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:24:23.574Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ff57f5d9-700d-4879-8668-6796efff5dcb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01C938rwaFJZBHRmCgr4bk6P","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Daily signed checkpoint of the spine head (SIGIL §6.3.6, WARDEN anchor).\n2\t\n3\tGenerates + persists a solo-owner Ed25519 keypair once (1-of-1 trust root), then signs\n4\tthe chain head so the whole spine is anchored: rewriting history would require forging\n5\tthe owner signature, and a shrunk/back-dated head is caught by the monotonic last_seq.\n6\t\n7\tNote: for a personal, single-owner, local-first system the owner's signing key lives\n8\tlocally (0600) — a deliberate simplification of the framework's \"keys never on the\n9\truntime host\" rule, appropriate to this threat model (§1.3). A hardware co-signer can be\n10\tadded later by raising the trust-root threshold.\n11\t\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport os\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\t\n18\t_MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA = 2   # head schema versions this build understands; a higher one -> \"upgrade sigil\"\n19\t\n20\tfrom ..config import HEAD_PATH, KEYS_DIR, OWNER_KEY_ID, SCOPE\n21\tfrom ..reuse import (\n22\t    AuthorizerKey,\n23\t    SignedChainHead,\n24\t    TrustRoot,\n25\t    generate_keypair,\n26\t    sign_head,\n27\t    verify_head,\n28\t)\n29\tfrom .atomicio import atomic_write_text\n30\tfrom .floor import Floor, advance_floor, check_floor, load_floor\n31\tfrom .store import SpineStore\n32\t\n33\t_PRIV = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.priv\"\n34\t_PUB = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.pub\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _atomic_write_text(path: Path, data: str) -> None:\n38\t    \"\"\"Durably + atomically replace `path` with `data` (FIX 3) — a reader sees either the whole old or\n39\t    the whole new head, never a torn one, and a crash leaves the previous valid head intact. Delegates to\n40\t    the one shared `spine.atomicio.atomic_write_text` so the head, the manifest, and every cutover use the\n41\t    same audited routine (no drift).\"\"\"\n42\t    atomic_write_text(path, data, prefix=\".head-\")\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef _owner_keys() -> tuple[str, str]:\n46\t    if not _PRIV.exists():\n47\t        KEYS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n48\t        kp = generate_keypair()\n49\t        _PRIV.write_text(kp.private_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n50\t        os.chmod(_PRIV, 0o600)\n51\t        _PUB.write_text(kp.public_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n52\t    return _PRIV.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip(), _PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef trust_root() -> TrustRoot:\n56\t    _, pub = _owner_keys()\n57\t    return TrustRoot(threshold=1, authorizers=[\n58\t        AuthorizerKey(key_id=OWNER_KEY_ID, name=\"owner\", public_key_b64=pub)])\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef checkpoint(store: SpineStore | None = None) -> SignedChainHead:\n62\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n63\t    priv, _ = _owner_keys()\n64\t    head = sign_head(store.entries(), engagement_slug=SCOPE, signers=[(OWNER_KEY_ID, priv)])\n65\t    _atomic_write_text(HEAD_PATH, head.model_dump_json())   # FIX 3: never leave a torn head on a crash\n66\t    # Advance the durable external anti-rollback floor to this just-committed head (upward-only). The head\n67\t    # is already durable above, so the floor never names a non-durable head. BEST-EFFORT: a stale/locked/\n68\t    # corrupt floor must not brick signing (consolidate + warden-anchor also route here) — and a\n69\t    # NOT-advanced floor only ever REJECTS MORE (it can never cause a false-CLEAN of the current state),\n70\t    # so warning is the fail-SAFE outcome. `sigil floor reset` re-seeds after a legitimate reset/restore.\n71\t    try:\n72\t        advance_floor(head)\n73\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — includes the DOWNWARD-refusal after a routine reset (intended)\n74\t        import sys\n75\t        print(f\"warning: durable anti-rollback floor not advanced ({e}); run `sigil floor reset` after a \"\n76\t              f\"deliberate reset/restore, else investigate\", file=sys.stderr)\n77\t    return head\n78\t\n79\t\n80\tdef classify_head(head: SignedChainHead, entries: list, tr: TrustRoot,\n81\t                  *, floor: Floor | None = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n82\t    \"\"\"Pure head/chain classification (no globals — testable in isolation), SNAPSHOT-AWARE for the\n83\t    cold-archive hard-prune. The live window is selected BY SEQ (`seq >= base_seq`), not by array prefix, so\n84\t    every crash-cutover state verifies clean. Failures are TAMPERING (front-truncation, tail-truncation, or\n85\t    a rewrite); a chain grown past the anchor is benign-stale. entry_count stays ABSOLUTE (base_count +\n86\t    live). For a v1 head (base_seq=0, base_count=0) this is BYTE-IDENTICAL to the pre-v2 semantics.\"\"\"\n87\t    base = head.base_count\n88\t    signed_live = head.entry_count - base\n89\t    live = [e for e in entries if e.seq >= head.base_seq]\n90\t    if len(live) < signed_live:\n91\t        return False, (f\"TAMPERING: live window has {len(live)} records but the signed head anchors \"\n92\t                       f\"{signed_live} (truncated/rolled back)\")\n93\t    if signed_live > 0:\n94\t        # LEFT-EDGE PIN — UNCONDITIONAL TAMPERING: the signed live window must start EXACTLY at base_seq and\n95\t        # link from base_prev_hash. This closes the false-CLEAN front-drop hole (the n<entry_count no-op\n96\t        # trap transposed to the left edge) — an attacker cannot drop the front of the live window. (Only\n97\t        # the window-SHAPE check is gated on signed_live>0; the SIGNATURE check below is not.)\n98\t        if live[0].seq != head.base_seq or live[0].prev_hash != head.base_prev_hash:\n99\t            return False, \"TAMPERING: live window does not start at the signed base (front-truncated/rolled back)\"\n100\t    # SIGNATURE / threshold check runs UNCONDITIONALLY — including signed_live == 0 (an empty live window, the\n101\t    # case a forged zero-anchor head presents). verify_head over an empty window still validates\n102\t    # head_hash==base_prev_hash, last_seq==base_seq, entry_count==base_count AND the owner Ed25519 signature,\n103\t    # so a forged UNSIGNED zero head is TAMPERING (the whole tamper-evidence point), while a legitimately\n104\t    # owner-signed empty spine still passes. (An earlier version gated this behind signed_live>0 and thereby\n105\t    # skipped signature verification for empty heads — a keyless tamper-alert-suppression fail-open.)\n106\t    ok, msg = verify_head(head, live[:signed_live], tr, genesis_prev=head.base_prev_hash)\n107\t    if not ok:\n108\t        return False, f\"TAMPERING: history rewritten at or below the signed head — {msg}\"\n109\t    # DURABLE EXTERNAL FLOOR (hard-prune C1) — runs AFTER the in-band signature check, so it only ever\n110\t    # ADDS a rejection: a VALIDLY-signed but STALE head (an attacker replays a genuinely-old owner-signed\n111\t    # head.json, or a routine reset shortens the spine) is caught here by the monotonic floor / meta-chain,\n112\t    # which the in-band signature cannot catch on its own. No floor -> pass (byte-identical to pre-floor).\n113\t    ok_floor, floor_msg = check_floor(head, floor)\n114\t    if not ok_floor:\n115\t        return False, f\"TAMPERING: {floor_msg}\"\n116\t    if len(live) > signed_live:\n117\t        return True, f\"anchors {signed_live} records; {len(live) - signed_live} appended since — run `sigil sign` to re-anchor\"\n118\t    return True, f\"anchors all {signed_live} records (current)\"\n119\t\n120\t\n121\tdef verify_checkpoint(store: SpineStore | None = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n122\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n123\t    if not HEAD_PATH.exists():\n124\t        return False, \"no signed head — run `sigil sign` to anchor the spine\"\n125\t    raw = HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n126\t    try:\n127\t        head = SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(raw)     # a future-schema head fails extra=\"forbid\" here\n128\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — never crash + never false-clean: distinguish \"too new\" from \"corrupt\"\n129\t        try:\n130\t            sv = int(json.loads(raw).get(\"schema_version\", 1))\n131\t        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):\n132\t            return False, \"corrupt head — cannot parse (run `sigil sign` to re-anchor)\"\n133\t        if sv > _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA:\n134\t            return False, f\"head schema v{sv} too new — upgrade sigil (never treated as clean)\"\n135\t        return False, \"corrupt head — cannot parse (run `sigil sign` to re-anchor)\"\n136\t    if head.schema_version > _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA:\n137\t        return False, f\"head schema v{head.schema_version} too new — upgrade sigil (never treated as clean)\"\n138\t    try:\n139\t        floor = load_floor()                                # None if absent -> byte-identical to pre-floor\n140\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a PRESENT-but-corrupt floor is suspicious; fail CLOSED, never clean\n141\t        return False, f\"durable anti-rollback floor unreadable — refuse to certify (possible tamper): {e}\"\n142\t    return classify_head(head, store.entries(), trust_root(), floor=floor)\n143\t"}]},"uuid":"1e11752e-55da-4025-84b0-de0b9f9eeba1","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:24:23.591Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ff57f5d9-700d-4879-8668-6796efff5dcb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Every agent record carries its actor, tier, governor decision + reason; this reconstructs that\n3\ttrail with each row cited to a spine seq. Read-only, A0 — it fabricates nothing, it only reports what\n4\tthe append-only log already proves (which is the whole point of C18).\"\"\"\n5\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n6\t\n7\tfrom collections import Counter\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\t\n10\tfrom .spine.store import SpineStore\n11\t\n12\t_DECISIONS = (\"auto\", \"queued\", \"denied\")\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef self_audit(store: SpineStore, *, agent: Optional[str] = None, since_seq: int = -1,\n16\t               limit: int = 500) -> List[dict]:\n17\t    \"\"\"Every governed agent action since `since_seq`, most-recent last, each cited. `agent` filters.\"\"\"\n18\t    rows: List[dict] = []\n19\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=since_seq):\n20\t        if r.source != \"agent\":\n21\t            continue\n22\t        if agent and r.actor != agent:\n23\t            continue\n24\t        p = r.payload\n25\t        dec = p.get(\"decision\")\n26\t        if dec not in _DECISIONS:\n27\t            continue\n28\t        rows.append({\n29\t            \"seq\": r.seq, \"entry_hash\": r.entry_hash, \"when\": r.ts, \"agent\": r.actor,\n30\t            \"kind\": r.kind, \"tier\": p.get(\"tier\"), \"decision\": dec,\n31\t            \"why\": p.get(\"governor\") or \"(recorded before the governor)\",\n32\t            \"what\": p.get(\"subject\") or p.get(\"summary\") or (r.text()[:80]),\n33\t            \"parent_id\": r.parent_id,\n34\t        })\n35\t    return rows[-limit:]\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef render_audit(rows: List[dict], *, agent: Optional[str] = None) -> str:\n39\t    who = agent or \"the agent mesh\"\n40\t    lines = [f\"# SIGIL self-audit — what {who} did, and why\", \"\"]\n41\t    if not rows:\n42\t        lines.append(\"- no governed agent actions on record for that window.\")\n43\t        return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n44\t    by_dec = Counter(r[\"decision\"] for r in rows)\n45\t    by_agent = Counter(r[\"agent\"] for r in rows)\n46\t    lines.append(f\"{len(rows)} action(s): \"\n47\t                 + \", \".join(f\"{n} {d}\" for d, n in by_dec.items()) + \".\")\n48\t    lines.append(\"by agent: \" + \", \".join(f\"{a} {n}\" for a, n in by_agent.most_common()) + \".\")\n49\t    lines.append(\"\")\n50\t    for r in rows[-40:]:\n51\t        lines.append(f\"- seq {r['seq']} [{r['tier']}/{r['decision']}] {r['agent']} · {r['kind']}: \"\n52\t {"parentUuid":"9fac1ebe-27e4-4659-ab4c-0bb2edbacfec","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01L1QzfdwoEEa7YXbXgLpugy","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Read-only operator dashboard (SIGIL §9.4, C39/C40) — a glanceable status surface over the spine:\n2\tkill-switch state, per-agent activity, the tiered decision breakdown (auto/queued/denied), the\n3\tpending approval queue, today's budget usage, and ingest lag. LOOPBACK, READ-ONLY, ZERO-IMPACT: it\n4\tonly reads the append-only log and writes nothing (same posture as the CRUCIBLE ops console). A\n5\tproper TUI/web view renders this same dict; here it renders to text.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom collections import Counter\n9\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n10\tfrom typing import Optional\n11\t\n12\tfrom .spine.store import SpineStore\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef snapshot(store: SpineStore, *, day_iso: Optional[str] = None, lookback: int = 300) -> dict:\n16\t    from .agents.approvals import pending\n17\t    from .governor import KillSwitch\n18\t    from .governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n19\t    head = store.next_seq - 1\n20\t    day = day_iso or datetime.now(timezone.utc).date().isoformat()\n21\t\n22\t    per_agent: Counter = Counter()\n23\t    decisions: Counter = Counter()\n24\t    interrupts_today: Counter = Counter()\n25\t    actions_today: Counter = Counter()\n26\t    last_checkpoint = -1\n27\t    last_consolidation = -1\n28\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n29\t        if r.kind == \"warden_checkpoint\":\n30\t            last_checkpoint = r.seq\n31\t        if r.kind in (\"entity\", \"contradiction\") and r.source == \"agent\":\n32\t            last_consolidation = max(last_consolidation, r.seq)\n33\t        if r.source == \"agent\" and (r.ts or \"\").startswith(day):\n34\t            # count only actions actually TAKEN (auto/queued), mirroring the enforced BudgetLedger —\n35\t            # denials must not inflate the usage panel (red-pen RP-2 honesty).\n36\t            if r.payload.get(\"decision\") in (\"auto\", \"queued\"):\n37\t                actions_today[r.actor] += 1\n38\t                if r.kind == \"event\":\n39\t                    interrupts_today[r.actor] += 1\n40\t        if r.source == \"agent\" and r.seq > head - lookback:\n41\t            per_agent[r.actor] += 1\n42\t            decisions[str(r.payload.get(\"decision\"))] += 1\n43\t\n44\t    pend = pending(store, owner_pubkey())\n45\t    return {\n46\t        \"head_seq\": head,\n47\t        \"kill_switch\": \"ENGAGED\" if KillSwitch(store).is_engaged() else \"released\",\n48\t        \"recent_by_agent\": dict(per_agent.most_common()),\n49\t        \"recent_decisions\": dict(decisions),\n50\t        \"pending_approvals\": [{\"seq\": r.seq, \"tier\": r.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": r.kind,\n51\t                               \"agent\": r.actor, \"subject\": r.payload.get(\"subject\")} for r in pend],\n52\t        \"budget_today\": {a: {\"actions\": actions_today[a], \"interrupts\": interrupts_today[a]}\n53\t                         for a in sorted(actions_today)},\n54\t        \"ingest_lag\": {\"head_seq\": head, \"last_checkpoint_seq\": last_checkpoint,\n55\t                       \"records_since_checkpoint\": (head - last_checkpoint) if last_checkpoint >= 0 else None,"}]},"uuid":"55fa253f-9430-43dd-b4aa-d2e2c44bdb8d","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:24:47.528Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9fac1ebe-27e4-4659-ab4c-0bb2edbacfec","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"e2de963c-f48f-4bf7-bed2-142cc0af31e8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YSmQCi6W5DX1jyZrGq3rFZ","type":"tool_result","content":"70\t    \"\"\"Read a CONTIGUOUS window of the owner's episodic spine in seq order — the raw record\n71\t    stream (messages, tool calls, commits, docs), not a semantic search. Use this to\n72\t    reconstruct *what happened* around a known point (e.g. after `memory_search` gives you a\n73\t    seq, read the seqs around it for context), or to page through a session/day.\n74\t\n75\t    Every record is tamper-evident (seq + entry_hash). Cite seqs when you answer.\n76\t\n77\t    Args:\n78\t        start_seq: first seq to return (inclusive, default 0 = beginning).\n79\t        end_seq: last seq (inclusive); default = newest.\n80\t        kind: optional filter (message | tool_call | tool_result | commit | document | session | decision | commitment | brief).\n81\t        limit: max records (default 25, hard cap 200).\n82\t    \"\"\"\n83\t    store = SpineStore()\n84\t    limit = max(1, min(int(limit), 200))\n85\t    hi = (store.next_seq - 1) if end_seq is None else int(end_seq)\n86\t    lo = max(0, int(start_seq))\n87\t    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n88\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=lo - 1):  # yields seq >= lo\n89\t        if r.seq > hi:\n90\t            break\n91\t        if kind and r.kind != kind:\n92\t            continue\n93\t        out.append({\n94\t            \"seq\": r.seq, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n95\t            \"when\": r.ts, \"session\": r.payload.get(\"session_id\"), \"project\": r.payload.get(\"project\"),\n96\t            \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n97\t            \"entry_hash\": r.entry_hash, \"text\": (r.text() or \"\")[:800],\n98\t        })\n99\t        if len(out) >= limit:\n100\t            break\n101\t    return {\"range\": {\"start_seq\": lo, \"end_seq\": hi}, \"kind\": kind, \"count\": len(out),\n102\t            \"records\": out,\n103\t            \"provenance\": \"Each record is a tamper-evident spine entry; cite its `seq` when you answer.\"}\n104\t\n105\t\n106\t@mcp.tool()\n107\tdef ingest_status(verify: bool = True) -> dict[str, Any]:\n108\t    \"\"\"Report the health of SIGIL memory: how much is stored, how much is embedded for\n109\t    semantic recall, and — with `verify` — whether the spine's integrity chain and signed\n110\t    head still hold (tamper-evidence). Use this to tell the owner how current/trustworthy\n111\t    their memory is before relying on it.\n112\t\n113\t    Args:\n114\t        verify: also run the (heavier) integrity + signed-head check (default true).\n115\t    \"\"\"\n116\t    store = SpineStore()\n117\t    vi = _idx()\n118\t    status: dict[str, Any] = {\n119\t        \"spine_records\": store.count(),\n120\t        \"next_seq\": store.next_seq,\n121\t        \"vectors_indexed\": vi.count(),\n122\t        \"last_indexed_seq\": vi.last_indexed_seq(),\n123\t        \"embedding_model\": EMBED_MODEL,\n124\t        \"qdrant_mode\": \"server\" if QDRANT_URL else \"local-embedded\","}]},"uuid":"f84290f0-a6a4-4373-9aa4-e82be8dda7bc","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:24:47.974Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e2de963c-f48f-4bf7-bed2-142cc0af31e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"11d8d7c0-144c-46c1-b04b-4ae09ea2acbb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01THmZT27iiopqcBP74Msshu","type":"tool_result","content":"280\t    store = SpineStore()\n281\t    ok, msg = store.verify()                       # chain: catches a tampered/deleted anchor record\n282\t    if not ok:\n283\t        print(f\"REJECTED: spine integrity failed — {msg}\", file=sys.stderr)\n284\t        sys.exit(2)\n285\t    hok, hmsg = verify_checkpoint(store)           # signed head: catches spine tail-truncation\n286\t    if not hok and \"TAMPER\" in hmsg.upper():\n287\t        print(f\"REJECTED: spine head tampered — {hmsg}\", file=sys.stderr)\n288\t        sys.exit(2)\n289\t    best_count, best_hash = 0, \"\"\n290\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n291\t        if r.kind == \"warden_checkpoint\" and r.payload.get(\"pubkey\") == a.pubkey:\n292\t            c = int(r.payload.get(\"count\", 0))\n293\t            if c >= best_count:\n294\t                best_count, best_hash = c, r.payload.get(\"head_hash\", \"\")\n295\t    print(_json.dumps({\"count\": best_count, \"head_hash\": best_hash}))\n296\t\n297\t\n298\tdef cmd_warden(a) -> None:\n299\t    \"\"\"Phase 6 governor controls (SIGIL §5): kill switch + per-kind promotion policy. Governance"}]},"uuid":"a57dca80-ff10-41e9-a108-eeb316a7b396","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:25:09.398Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"11d8d7c0-144c-46c1-b04b-4ae09ea2acbb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"99bf5c93-f6aa-4a2f-8a78-a31b396d19c0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EV2cUQF5naN85PXNQfqVCC","type":"tool_result","content":"20\t\n21\tEGRESS_SIGNAL = \"vision.egress\"\n22\tFRONTIER_TOOL = \"vision.frontier.upload\"   # classified A2 by the WARDEN oracle (contains \"upload\")\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef egress_token(frame_sha256: str, question: str) -> str:\n26\t    \"\"\"A stable id binding an approval to exactly this (frame, question) upload.\"\"\"\n27\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{frame_sha256}|{question or ''}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef egress_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n31\t    \"\"\"True iff `seq` is an egress request bound to `token` AND carries a VERIFIED approval by the\n32\t    owner OR an owner-authorized device. Fail-closed: wrong token, no approval, denied, or an\n33\t    approval of a different seq → False.\"\"\"\n34\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n35\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != EGRESS_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"egress_token\") != token:\n36\t        return False\n37\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n38\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n39\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n40\t        p = r.payload\n41\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n42\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n43\t            return True\n44\t    return False\n45\t"}]},"uuid":"a41ba8e8-3669-488a-92b8-6c8aaef22500","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:25:09.410Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"99bf5c93-f6aa-4a2f-8a78-a31b396d19c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"194a7684-bce3-4feb-b8e5-70355e083af2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q4BqjeUvgSbpZDWHzKF3HT","type":"tool_result","content":"15\t@runtime_checkable\n16\tclass Watcher(Protocol):\n17\t    def poll(self) -> List[dict]: ...   # each: {kind, summary, salience(0..1), detail?}\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tclass SpineActivityWatcher:\n21\t    \"\"\"Salient signals derived from the spine: a burst of new commits, and any pending\n22\t    self-contradiction the consolidation flagged.\"\"\"\n23\t    def __init__(self, store, since_seq: int, commit_burst: int = 3):\n24\t        self.store, self.since_seq, self.commit_burst = store, since_seq, commit_burst\n25\t\n26\t    def poll(self) -> List[dict]:\n27\t        out: List[dict] = []\n28\t        commits = sum(1 for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=self.since_seq) if r.kind == \"commit\")\n29\t        if commits >= self.commit_burst:\n30\t            out.append({\"kind\": \"commits\", \"summary\": f\"{commits} new commits since last check\",\n31\t                        \"salience\": min(1.0, 0.4 + commits / 20.0)})\n32\t        from ..consolidate.queries import pending_contradictions\n33\t        for c in pending_contradictions(self.store, limit=5):\n34\t            out.append({\"kind\": \"contradiction\", \"summary\": f\"unresolved contradiction on '{c.get('subject')}'\",\n35\t                        \"salience\": 0.9, \"detail\": c.get(\"conflicting_seqs\")})\n36\t        return out\n37\t\n38\t\n39\tclass SystemHealthWatcher:"}]},"uuid":"e6121219-964a-41cd-8d5b-61b3ecf6e375","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:25:56.270Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"194a7684-bce3-4feb-b8e5-70355e083af2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

gate (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — a near-verbatim mirror of `perception/egress.py`, but for web\n2\tACTIONS. `action_token = sha256(service|step|url|page_sha256|field_binding)` binds an approval to ONE\n3\texact action — the destination URL is IN the preimage (so an approval can never be rebound to a\n4\tdifferent URL, even one serving identical bytes), alongside the page hash (a page-change aborts) and\n5\tthe field binding (a vault-version bump / literal edit aborts). `action_approved` requires a VERIFIED\n6\towner/authorized-device approval whose signed `target_seq` IS that step — a replay onto a different\n7\taction, a wrong token, an unsigned/denied approval → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n11\tfrom .approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n12\tfrom .approvals import verify_approval\n13\t\n14\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"web.actor.step\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef action_token(service: str, step_kind: str, url: str, page_sha256: str, field_binding: str) -> str:\n18\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{step_kind}|{url}|{page_sha256}|{field_binding}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef action_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n22\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n23\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"action_token\") != token:\n24\t        return False\n25\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n26\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n27\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n28\t        p = r.payload\n29\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n30\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n31\t            return True\n32\t    return False\n33\t"}]},"uuid":"e9249ede-18b3-4888-bc6c-73d52ef994b0","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:25:56.598Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"480f4432-8c76-4a01-aeb6-e4335a500275","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"cf701ab6-dc2b-44fa-88bb-8bbae9fbf8dd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017rS1TnYJdt3k2s3YBXRcsE","type":"tool_result","content":"185\t            if seq is not None:\n186\t                queued.append(seq)\n187\t        return queued, res\n188\t\n189\t    # --- execute: verified approval → re-fetch → block-before-act → re-derive token → last-instant act\n190\t    def execute(self, step_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n191\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n192\t        rec = self.store.get(step_seq)\n193\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL:\n194\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {step_seq} is not a web-actor step\")\n195\t            return res\n196\t        token = rec.payload[\"action_token\"]\n197\t        if not action_approved(self.store, step_seq, token, self._tp()):\n198\t            res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} has no verified owner approval — NO request made\")\n199\t            return res\n200\t        # SINGLE-SHOT: one approval authorises exactly ONE action. A step that already produced an applied\n201\t        # record is refused (the append-only approval never expires, so without this guard one signature\n202\t        # would authorise unbounded repeats). Mirrors the Operator's single-shot undo (BLOCK-6).\n203\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=step_seq):\n204\t            if (r.payload.get(\"signal\") == ACTION_SIGNAL and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\"\n205\t                    and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == step_seq):\n206\t                res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} was already executed (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay (per-action)\")\n207\t                return res\n208\t        try:                                                 # executable step lives OFF the spine (0700)\n209\t            j = json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"journal\"]).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n210\t            service, kind, url, fields = j[\"service\"], j[\"kind\"], j[\"url\"], j[\"fields\"]\n211\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n212\t            res.notes.append(\"step journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n213\t            return res\n214\t        if not self.scope.origin_allowed(url):               # re-check scope at execute (belt-and-suspenders)"}]},"uuid":"fcfa791a-1cc9-451c-ad8b-b94cf0eb3a7e","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:26:07.400Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cf701ab6-dc2b-44fa-88bb-8bbae9fbf8dd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"bcf79a2f-7da6-49ba-8774-14de72de24e7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LXzdsHUqFwHS79dDLHZnzV","type":"tool_result","content":"20\t\n21\tclass PushNotifier:\n22\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, since_seq: Optional[int] = None):\n23\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n24\t        # Start at the current head (only NEW items push) unless an explicit resume cursor is supplied.\n25\t        # `poll()` advances this cursor over EVERY record it walks, so a subsequent poll never re-emits.\n26\t        self.cursor = since_seq if since_seq is not None else self.store.next_seq - 1\n27\t\n28\t    def poll(self) -> List[dict]:\n29\t        \"\"\"New pushes since the last poll: one per newly-QUEUED A2/A3 item, minimal fields only.\n30\t\n31\t        ONE pass over the spine (`iter_records(since_seq=cursor)`); each record's tier/decision/status\n32\t        is read from its OWN payload — no per-event `store.get` re-scan (the audit-flagged double read).\n33\t        The cursor advances over every walked record so the next poll starts strictly after it.\"\"\"\n34\t        out: List[dict] = []\n35\t        for rec in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=self.cursor):\n36\t            self.cursor = rec.seq\n37\t            p = rec.payload or {}\n38\t            if (p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == \"awaiting-approval\"\n39\t                    and p.get(\"tier\") in (\"A2\", \"A3\")):\n40\t                out.append({\"seq\": rec.seq, \"tier\": p.get(\"tier\"), \"kind\": rec.kind})   # no subject/secret\n41\t        return out\n42\t"}]},"uuid":"c692903b-0cd5-4ae2-bd52-7fb2a3cdf6c1","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:26:16.051Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bcf79a2f-7da6-49ba-8774-14de72de24e7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f262d36c-6e87-4c36-bfe5-dd0bd5a052f5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0188dMhFKGNsgSjyniydPGQc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"STEWARD (SIGIL §4.7) — personal operations: the morning brief, the commitment ledger,\n2\trecurring admin. Ceiling A2 (calendar writes queue until trust promotes them). The morning\n3\tBRIEF is the Phase-3 acceptance deliverable: an unprompted, prioritized sitrep composed only\n4\tfrom grounded memory — due/overdue commitments first, then open threads, then flagged\n5\tcontradictions, then a recent-activity summary. Every line is cited to a spine seq.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom collections import Counter\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..consolidate.queries import due_commitments, open_threads, pending_contradictions\n11\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tdef _recent_activity(store, since_seq: int) -> Counter:\n15\t    c: Counter = Counter()\n16\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=since_seq):\n17\t        c[r.kind] += 1\n18\t    return c\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef compose_brief(store, *, date_label: str = \"today\", lookback: int = 400) -> str:\n22\t    threads = open_threads(store, limit=8)\n23\t    due = due_commitments(store, limit=8)\n24\t    contras = pending_contradictions(store, limit=5)\n25\t    head = store.next_seq - 1\n26\t    activity = _recent_activity(store, max(-1, head - lookback))\n27\t\n28\t    lines = [f\"# SIGIL morning brief — {date_label}\", \"Good morning. Here's where things stand.\", \"\"]\n29\t\n30\t    lines.append(f\"## Commitments ({len(due)})\")\n31\t    if due:\n32\t        for c in sorted(due, key=lambda d: str(d.get(\"due\"))):\n33\t            lines.append(f\"- due {c['due']} — {(c.get('text') or '')[:80]}  (owner {c.get('owner')}, seq {c['seq']})\")\n34\t    else:\n35\t        lines.append(\"- none with a due date on record.\")\n36\t\n37\t    lines.append(f\"\\n## Open threads ({len(threads)})\")\n38\t    if threads:\n39\t        for t in threads[:8]:\n40\t            lines.append(f\"- [{t['kind']}] {(t.get('text') or '')[:90]}  (seq {t['seq']})\")\n41\t    else:\n42\t        lines.append(\"- nothing open — the consolidation pass has surfaced no live decisions/commitments yet.\")\n43\t\n44\t    if contras:\n45\t        lines.append(f\"\\n## Flagged for review ({len(contras)})\")\n46\t        for x in contras:\n47\t            lines.append(f\"- {x.get('subject')}: conflicting decisions at seqs {x.get('conflicting_seqs')}\")\n48\t\n49\t    alerts = [r for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=max(-1, head - 60))\n50\t              if r.kind == \"event\" and r.actor == \"SENTINEL\"]\n51\t    if alerts:\n52\t        lines.append(f\"\\n## Alerts (SENTINEL, {len(alerts)})\")\n53\t        for a in alerts[-6:]:\n54\t            lines.append(f\"- {a.payload.get('summary')}  (salience {a.payload.get('salience')})\")\n55\t\n56\t    # BASTION defensive posture (§4.8): cert/CVE/uptime findings surface here (Phase-5 acceptance).\n57\t    # Dedup to the LATEST record per finding IDENTITY (asset, check, CVE) — the CVE discriminator keeps\n58\t    # N concurrent CVEs distinct (RP-2) — then DROP any whose latest state is `resolved`, so a fixed\n59\t    # problem never lingers in the brief as current (RP-1/RP-5).\n60\t    posture: dict = {}\n61\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=max(-1, head - 400)):\n62\t        if r.kind == \"finding\" and r.actor == \"BASTION\":\n63\t            posture[(r.payload.get(\"asset\"), r.payload.get(\"check\"), r.payload.get(\"cve\"))] = r\n64\t    active = [r for r in posture.values() if not r.payload.get(\"resolved\")]\n65\t    if active:\n66\t        order = {\"critical\": 0, \"high\": 1, \"medium\": 2, \"low\": 3}\n67\t        active.sort(key=lambda r: (order.get(str(r.payload.get(\"severity\")), 4), r.seq))\n68\t        lines.append(f\"\\n## Infrastructure posture (BASTION, {len(active)})\")\n69\t        for r in active[:8]:\n70\t            p = r.payload\n71\t            lines.append(f\"- [{p.get('severity', '?')}] {p.get('summary')}  \"\n72\t                         f\"({p.get('quote')}, seq {r.seq})\")\n73\t\n74\t    lines.append(\"\\n## Recent activity\")\n75\t    interesting = {\"commit\": \"commits\", \"decision\": \"decisions\", \"commitment\": \"commitments\",\n76\t                   \"message\": \"messages\", \"event\": \"events\", \"finding\": \"findings\"}\n77\t    summary = \", \".join(f\"{activity[k]} {label}\" for k, label in interesting.items() if activity.get(k))\n78\t    lines.append(f\"- last {lookback} records: {summary or 'quiet'}.\")\n79\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tclass Steward(Agent):\n83\t    name = \"STEWARD\"\n84\t    mandate = \"run the life layer: calendar, briefs, commitments, recurring admin\"\n85\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n86\t\n87\t    def run(self, *, date_label: str = \"today\") -> AgentResult:\n88\t        text = compose_brief(self.store, date_label=date_label)\n89\t        # the brief is a reversible internal write → A1, auto.\n90\t        return self._dispatch([Proposal(\"brief\", {\"text\": text, \"subject\": \"morning brief\"}, Tier.A1)])\n91\t\n92\t    def brief_text(self, *, date_label: str = \"today\") -> str:\n93\t        return compose_brief(self.store, date_label=date_label)\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"b00ff0e1-1b77-41ab-86df-67f536d16497","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:26:16.386Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f262d36c-6e87-4c36-bfe5-dd0bd5a052f5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f3acac8b-3e2a-493d-80db-f10cae3896a9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GiXdebGRzq5yJTP8ddDWtd","type":"tool_result","content":"228\t        from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n229\t        from .approvals import SIGNAL as APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n230\t        from .approvals import verify_approval\n231\t        tp = self._tp()\n232\t        devices = authorized_devices(self.store, tp)   # the owner + any owner-authorized device may approve\n233\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=plan_seq):\n234\t            p = r.payload\n235\t            if (p.get(\"signal\") == APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == plan_seq\n236\t                    and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, tp, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n237\t                return True\n238\t        return False\n239\t\n240\t    def execute(self, plan_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n241\t        rec = self.store.get(plan_seq)\n242\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n243\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != \"operator.plan\":\n244\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {plan_seq} is not an operator plan\")\n245\t            return res\n246\t        try:\n247\t            steps = [Step(**s) for s in json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"plan_journal\"]).read_text())[\"steps\"]]\n248\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n249\t            res.notes.append(\"plan journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")"}]},"uuid":"3bed2f5f-5203-4f6f-b922-3f4c5d1a5d54","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:26:26.776Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f3acac8b-3e2a-493d-80db-f10cae3896a9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

The `cve` discriminator keeps N concurrent CVEs\n244\t        on one manifest distinct (RP-2) and lets a re-run resolve exactly the right prior finding.\"\"\"\n245\t        return (payload.get(\"asset\"), payload.get(\"check\"), payload.get(\"cve\"))\n246\t\n247\t    def _resolutions(self, fired_keys: set) -> List[Proposal]:\n248\t        \"\"\"For any (asset,check,cve) we flagged before that this run did NOT re-flag — and whose asset\n249\t        we actually re-assessed — emit a `resolved` supersession, so the brief stops presenting a fixed\n250\t        problem as current (RP-1/RP-5). A resolution supersedes the stale finding (latest wins).\"\"\"\n251\t        assessed = {a.name for a in self.inventory}\n252\t        latest: dict = {}\n253\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n254\t            if r.kind == \"finding\" and r.actor == self.name:\n255\t                latest[self._key(r.payload)] = r        # iter is seq-ascending → last seen = newest\n256\t        props: List[Proposal] = []\n257\t        for key, r in latest.items():\n258\t            if r.payload.get(\"resolved\"):\n259\t                continue                                # already resolved — don't churn\n260\t            asset_name, check, cve = key\n261\t            if asset_name in assessed and key not in fired_keys:\n262\t                props.append(Proposal(\"finding\", {"}]},"uuid":"22c1e4f9-18c0-401c-bf9f-94fd307097b7","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:26:37.197Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bc681593-38d0-42bf-a0eb-2f40acdc8845","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"b3be2161-78aa-40fe-934e-67a56aa150ed","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QPiEFwoKSyF2SPNbLfc9G6","type":"tool_result","content":"48\t\n49\tdef _accumulate(store: SpineStore):\n50\t    \"\"\"One pass over the spine → structural entity dicts (deterministic, extraction-free).\"\"\"\n51\t    sessions: dict[str, dict] = {}\n52\t    documents: dict[str, dict] = {}\n53\t    commits: dict[str, dict] = {}\n54\t    max_seq = -1\n55\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n56\t        max_seq = r.seq\n57\t        proj = normalize_project(r.payload.get(\"project\") or \"\")\n58\t        k = r.kind\n59\t        if k in _SESSION_KINDS:\n60\t            sid = r.payload.get(\"session_id\")\n61\t            if not sid:\n62\t                continue\n63\t            s = sessions.get(sid)\n64\t            if s is None:\n65\t                s = sessions[sid] = {"}]},"uuid":"801f18b9-c77d-40ab-a9da-958d8b018c24","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:26:37.951Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b3be2161-78aa-40fe-934e-67a56aa150ed","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"9e1c599a-72b0-42f4-be8e-cbfe1125e209","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011vd656ZChWbmQviEJUivRK","type":"tool_result","content":"25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef recall(store: SpineStore, subject: str) -> Optional[dict]:\n28\t    \"\"\"The most recent grounded sighting of `subject`, or None. Grounded = every salient token of the\n29\t    subject appears in that frame's captured OCR text (the authoritative ground truth).\"\"\"\n30\t    subj = salient(subject)\n31\t    if not subj:\n32\t        return None\n33\t    latest = None\n34\t    latest_line = None\n35\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n36\t        if r.kind != \"event\" or r.payload.get(\"signal\") != PERCEPTION_SIGNAL:\n37\t            continue\n38\t        line = _grounded_line(subj, r.payload.get(\"captured_text\") or \"\")\n39\t        if line is not None:                  # subject co-located on a real OCR line → a grounded sighting\n40\t            latest, latest_line = r, line     # seq-ascending iteration → last match is most recent\n41\t    if latest is None:\n42\t        return None\n43\t    return {\n44\t        \"seq\": latest.seq, \"entry_hash\": latest.entry_hash, \"when\": latest.ts,\n45\t        \"frame_sha256\": latest.payload.get(\"frame_sha256\"),\n46\t        \"quote\": latest_line,                 # verbatim OCR line that actually contains the subject\n47\t    }\n48\t"}]},"uuid":"e1437566-7b1a-4fdb-a8ae-bdd244013951","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:27:05.283Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9e1c599a-72b0-42f4-be8e-cbfe1125e209","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"4c7893ec-6df5-490f-af56-92b077c60c31","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ToiFuDYj2b7KcZGh4RtTLF","type":"tool_result","content":"65\tdef run_consolidation(provider: ExtractionProvider | None = None, *, store: SpineStore | None = None,\n66\t                      since_seq: int | None = None, batch_size: int = _BATCH, dry_run: bool = False,\n67\t                      do_brief: bool = True, sign: bool = True,\n68\t                      save_cursor: bool = True) -> ConsolidationReport:\n69\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n70\t    provider = provider or HeuristicProvider()\n71\t    since = since_seq if since_seq is not None else _load_cursor()\n72\t    head_before = store.next_seq - 1                       # never feed our own promotions\n73\t    window = [r for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=since)\n74\t              if r.seq <= head_before and r.kind in _FEED_KINDS]\n75\t\n76\t    admitted = []\n77\t    for batch in _chunks(window, batch_size):\n78\t        window_seqs = {r.seq for r in batch}               # exactly what the extractor saw\n79\t        for cand in provider.extract(batch):\n80\t            admitted.append((cand, admit(cand, window_seqs, store)))\n81\t\n82\t    if dry_run or not window:\n83\t        g = sum(1 for _, v in admitted if v.grounded)\n84\t        return ConsolidationReport("}]},"uuid":"e165a64b-fe22-4159-bb14-921b2e16eee7","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:27:05.815Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4c7893ec-6df5-490f-af56-92b077c60c31","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"41d5b18c-a420-4505-9ca8-c0a803d46cab","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016zavQMP6owHiEaHDB6SwKq","type":"tool_result","content":"78\t            raise ValueError(\"signing device is not authorized\")\n79\t        if not verify_approval(_Rec(payload), self.trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=authorized):\n80\t            raise ValueError(\"approval signature invalid\")\n81\t        sig, tgt = payload.get(\"sig\"), payload.get(\"target_seq\")\n82\t        # Best-effort dedup OUTSIDE the append lock (a rare concurrent double-record is benign; holding the\n83\t        # lock across the scan would stall every writer). A FULL-SPINE scan — approvals carry no freshness\n84\t        # field to bound a rotated captured-body pool, so a bounded window would re-open a bloat sink; the\n85\t        # full scan keeps replay-bloat bounded to the number of DISTINCT captured bodies, forever. Approvals\n86\t        # are human-paced, so O(spine) here (off the lock) is imperceptible.\n87\t        for r in self.store.iter_records():\n88\t            p = r.payload\n89\t            if p.get(\"signal\") == APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"pubkey\") == payload.get(\"pubkey\") and p.get(\"sig\") == sig:\n90\t                return r.seq                            # already recorded — idempotent\n91\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n92\t                                 payload={**payload, \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}, supersedes_id=tgt)\n93\t\n94\t    def submit_arm_request(self, request: dict, *, now: Optional[float] = None) -> int:\n95\t        \"\"\"Record a DEVICE-signed gesture arm request (the transport layer) — ONLY if the signing device"}]},"uuid":"fd6de8fb-49ce-45df-9a9a-92a890841158","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:27:23.534Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"41d5b18c-a420-4505-9ca8-c0a803d46cab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"b7fec30b-c8f1-44fb-aa90-5588842b58cc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"c8b44b39-e482-4e27-9bc3-44110e5fca8b","agentId":"a46cdac0d4f71d6c1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01J3PrPKQAVPCCZVJo81wnDU","type":"tool_result","content":"128\t        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n129\t            raise ValueError(\"invalid arm timestamp\")\n130\t        if not math.isfinite(ts) or not (abs(now - ts) <= ARM_FRESHNESS):\n131\t            raise ValueError(\"stale or non-finite arm request — refused (not recorded)\")\n132\t        sig = request.get(\"sig\")\n133\t        # Bounded-window dedup OUTSIDE the append lock: collapses a rapid within-freshness replay flood\n134\t        # (a flood is always recent). Aged-out replays are already refused above, so this need not scan\n135\t        # the whole spine to bound bloat.\n136\t        for r in self.store.tail(_DEDUP_WINDOW):\n137\t            p = r.payload\n138\t            if p.get(\"signal\") == ARM_REQUEST and p.get(\"pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"sig\") == sig:\n139\t                return r.seq                            # already recorded — idempotent\n140\t        # record ONLY the signed core + pubkey + sig (never `**request` — no unsigned extras persisted)\n141\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n142\t                                 payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"],\n143\t                                          \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"], \"ts\": core[\"ts\"],\n144\t                                          \"ttl_seconds\": core[\"ttl_seconds\"], \"pubkey\": pub, \"sig\": sig,\n145\t                                          \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})"}]},"uuid":"ea3234e4-6048-418e-a7de-ae526fceddf3","timestamp":"2026-07-19T21:27:52.188Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b7fec30b-c8f1-44fb-aa90-5588842b58cc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
